    for i, col in enumerate(table.tableColumns, 1):
        col.name = str(ws.cell(row=row, column=i).value)
        if col.name in formulas:
            col.calculatedColumnFormula = TableFormula(attr_text=formulas[col.name])


def _uzs(target, usd_rate):
    if target.our_price is None:
        return None
    if target.currency == "USD":
        return round(target.our_price * usd_rate) if usd_rate else None
    return target.our_price


PIVOT_PLACEHOLDERS = (
    ("Pivot by category", "Average market price vs baseline, by category and market"),
    ("Pivot price matrix", "Cheapest price by product and market"),
    ("Pivot market bands", "Offers per heat band, by market"),
)


def build_workbook(r: Research, path: str, pivot_placeholders: bool = False) -> dict:
    """Write the workbook. Returns the layout facts the pivot step needs.

    `pivot_placeholders=True` adds empty 'Pivot ...' sheets for the raw-OOXML pivot writer
    (excel/ooxml_pivot.py) to land on. The COM driver creates its own sheets: leave this False.
    """
    markets = list(r.markets)
    nm = len(markets)
    n = len(r.targets)
    if not n or not nm:
        raise ValueError("a workbook needs at least one product and one market")
    wb = Workbook()
    wb.calculation = CalcProperties(fullCalcOnLoad=True)
    wb.properties.title = r.title
    wb.properties.creator = "narxnoma"
    wb.properties.subject = "Market research: Offers / Products / Markets tables, Prices heat map"

    ws_start = wb.active
    ws_start.title = "Start"
    ws_prices = wb.create_sheet("Prices")
    ws_heat = wb.create_sheet("Heatmap")
    if pivot_placeholders:
        for name, title in PIVOT_PLACEHOLDERS:
            ph = wb.create_sheet(name)
            ph["A1"], ph["A1"].font = title, Font(bold=True, size=14)
            ph["A2"], ph["A2"].font = (
                (
                    "Built by Excel when this file is opened (refresh on load). "
                    "Data > Refresh All after edits."
                ),
                Font(italic=True, color="808080"),
            )
    ws_off = wb.create_sheet("Offers")
    ws_prod = wb.create_sheet("Products")
    ws_mkt = wb.create_sheet("Markets")
    ws_links = wb.create_sheet("Links")
    ws_set = wb.create_sheet("Settings")
    ws_notes = wb.create_sheet("Notes")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ Settings
    _header(ws_set, 1, ["Setting", "Value", "What it does"], [34, 16, 90])
    settings = [
        (
            "Par band (+/-)",
            0.02,
            "cfg_par",
            "Differences inside this band count as 'about equal' (no colour).",
        ),
        ("Moderate difference from", 0.10, "cfg_mid", "Medium red / green from here."),
        (
            "Strong difference from",
            0.20,
            "cfg_sat",
            "Darkest red / green from here; the Heatmap colour scale saturates here.",
        ),
        (
            "Baseline",
            "Our price",
            "cfg_baseline",
            "What every delta is measured against: 'Our price' or 'Market median'. "
            "A product with no price of ours always falls back to the median.",
        ),
    ]
    for i, (label, val, name, note) in enumerate(settings, 2):
        ws_set.cell(row=i, column=1, value=label)
        c = ws_set.cell(row=i, column=2, value=val)
        c.fill = INPUT_FILL
        if name != "cfg_baseline":
            c.number_format = "0%"
        ws_set.cell(row=i, column=3, value=note).alignment = Alignment(
            wrap_text=True, vertical="top"
        )
        wb.defined_names[name] = DefinedName(name, attr_text=f"Settings!$B${i}")
    dv = DataValidation(
        type="decimal",
        operator="between",
        formula1="0",
        formula2="1",
        showErrorMessage=True,
        errorTitle="Threshold",
        error="Enter a fraction between 0 and 1 (0.1 = 10%).",
    )
    ws_set.add_data_validation(dv)
    dv.add("B2:B4")
    dv2 = DataValidation(
        type="list", formula1='"Our price,Market median"', allow_blank=False, showErrorMessage=True
    )
    ws_set.add_data_validation(dv2)
    dv2.add("B5")
    ws_set["A8"] = "Heat-map legend (market price vs baseline)"
    ws_set["A8"].font = Font(bold=True)
    legend = [
        ("cheap3", "Market much cheaper (<= -strong)"),
        ("cheap2", "Market cheaper (<= -moderate)"),
        ("cheap1", "Market slightly cheaper"),
        (None, "About equal (inside the par band)"),
        ("dear1", "Market slightly dearer"),
        ("dear2", "Market dearer (>= +moderate)"),
        ("dear3", "Market much dearer (>= +strong)"),
    ]
    for i, (key, text) in enumerate(legend, 9):
        sw = ws_set.cell(row=i, column=1, value=text)
        if key:
            sw.fill = PatternFill("solid", fgColor=HEAT[key])
    ws_set.cell(row=16, column=1, value="Bold border = the cheapest price in its row").font = Font(
        bold=True
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------ Products (inputs + per-product facts)
    # column order is fixed up front: later formulas address columns by header name
    extra_keys = sorted({k for t in r.targets for k in t.extra})
    p_in = [
        "Product",
        "Category",
        "Our price",
        "Currency",
        "Our price original",
        "Note",
    ] + extra_keys
    p_calc = ["Baseline", "Offers", "Cheapest", "Highest", "Average", "Ours vs cheapest"]
    p_cols = p_in + p_calc
    pc = {name: i for i, name in enumerate(p_cols, 1)}
    _header(
        ws_prod,
        1,
        p_cols,
        [52, 16, 16, 9, 16, 28] + [18] * len(extra_keys) + [16, 9, 16, 16, 16, 16],
    )
    prices_median_col = 3 + nm + 4  # Prices: A B C | markets | Baseline Matched Min Median ...
    for i, t in enumerate(r.targets, 2):
        ws_prod.cell(row=i, column=pc["Product"], value=t.name)
        ws_prod.cell(row=i, column=pc["Category"], value=t.category or "")
        up = _uzs(t, r.usd_rate)
        c = ws_prod.cell(row=i, column=pc["Our price"], value=up)
        c.number_format, c.fill = MONEY, INPUT_FILL
        ws_prod.cell(row=i, column=pc["Category"]).fill = INPUT_FILL
        ws_prod.cell(row=i, column=pc["Currency"], value=t.currency)
        if t.our_price is not None:
            ws_prod.cell(
                row=i, column=pc["Our price original"], value=t.our_price
            ).number_format = "#,##0.##"
        ws_prod.cell(row=i, column=pc["Note"], value=t.note)
        for k in extra_keys:
            ws_prod.cell(row=i, column=pc[k], value=t.extra.get(k))
        mc = L(prices_median_col)
        f = {
            "Baseline": (
                f'=IF(AND(cfg_baseline="Our price",ISNUMBER({tr("tblProducts", "Our price")}),'
                f"N({tr('tblProducts', 'Our price')})>0),{tr('tblProducts', 'Our price')},"
                f"IFERROR(INDEX(Prices!${mc}:${mc},"
                f'MATCH({tr("tblProducts", "Product")},Prices!$A:$A,0)),""))'
            ),
            "Offers": f"=COUNTIFS(tblOffers[Product],{tr('tblProducts', 'Product')})",
            "Cheapest": (
                f'=IF({tr("tblProducts", "Offers")}=0,"",_xlfn.MINIFS(tblOffers[Price],'
                f"tblOffers[Product],{tr('tblProducts', 'Product')}))"
            ),
            "Highest": (
                f'=IF({tr("tblProducts", "Offers")}=0,"",_xlfn.MAXIFS(tblOffers[Price],'
                f"tblOffers[Product],{tr('tblProducts', 'Product')}))"
            ),
            "Average": (
                f'=IF({tr("tblProducts", "Offers")}=0,"",AVERAGEIFS(tblOffers[Price],'
                f"tblOffers[Product],{tr('tblProducts', 'Product')}))"
            ),
            "Ours vs cheapest": (
                f"=IF(AND(ISNUMBER({tr('tblProducts', 'Our price')}),"
                f"ISNUMBER({tr('tblProducts', 'Cheapest')})),"
                f'{tr("tblProducts", "Our price")}/{tr("tblProducts", "Cheapest")}-1,"")'
            ),
        }
        for name in p_calc:
            c = ws_prod.cell(row=i, column=pc[name], value=f[name])
            c.number_format = PCT if name == "Ours vs cheapest" else MONEY
    prod_last = n + 1
    t_prod = _table(ws_prod, "tblProducts", f"A1:{L(len(p_cols))}{prod_last}")
    _calc_columns(ws_prod, t_prod, {k: ws_prod.cell(row=2, column=pc[k]).value[1:] for k in p_calc})
    ws_prod.freeze_panes = "B2"

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ Offers (the pivot source)
    o_cols = [
        "Product",
        "Category",
        "Market",
        "Listing",
        "Price",
        "Availability",
        "Score",
        "Name delta",
        "URL",
        "Fetched",
        "Baseline",
        "Delta vs baseline",
        "Cheapest in product",
        "Band",
    ]
    oc = {name: i for i, name in enumerate(o_cols, 1)}
    _header(ws_off, 1, o_cols, [52, 16, 12, 56, 14, 14, 8, 36, 50, 20, 14, 12, 11, 26])
    order = {t.name: i for i, t in enumerate(r.targets)}
    offers = sorted(
        r.offers,
        key=lambda o: (
            order.get(o.target, 0),
            markets.index(o.market) if o.market in markets else 99,
            o.price,
        ),
    )
    o_f = {
        "Category": (
            f"=IFERROR(INDEX(tblProducts[Category],MATCH({tr('tblOffers', 'Product')},"
            f'tblProducts[Product],0)),"")'
        ),
        "Baseline": (
            f"=IFERROR(INDEX(tblProducts[Baseline],MATCH({tr('tblOffers', 'Product')},"
            f'tblProducts[Product],0)),"")'
        ),
        "Delta vs baseline": (
            f"=IF(AND(ISNUMBER({tr('tblOffers', 'Baseline')}),"
            f"N({tr('tblOffers', 'Baseline')})>0),"
            f'{tr("tblOffers", "Price")}/{tr("tblOffers", "Baseline")}-1,"")'
        ),
        "Cheapest in product": (
            f"=IF({tr('tblOffers', 'Price')}=_xlfn.MINIFS(tblOffers[Price],"
            f"tblOffers[Product],{tr('tblOffers', 'Product')}),1,0)"
        ),
    }
    d = tr("tblOffers", "Delta vs baseline")
    o_f["Band"] = (
        f'=IF({d}="","",IF({d}<=-cfg_sat,"{BANDS[0]}",IF({d}<=-cfg_mid,"{BANDS[1]}",'
        f'IF({d}<-cfg_par,"{BANDS[2]}",IF({d}<=cfg_par,"{BANDS[3]}",IF({d}<cfg_mid,"{BANDS[4]}",'
        f'IF({d}<cfg_sat,"{BANDS[5]}","{BANDS[6]}")))))))'
    )
    fetched_default = r.run_at[:19].replace("T", " ")
    for i, o in enumerate(offers, 2):
        ws_off.cell(row=i, column=oc["Product"], value=o.target)
        ws_off.cell(row=i, column=oc["Market"], value=o.market)
        ws_off.cell(row=i, column=oc["Listing"], value=o.listing_name)
        ws_off.cell(row=i, column=oc["Price"], value=o.price).number_format = MONEY
        ws_off.cell(row=i, column=oc["Availability"], value=o.availability)
        ws_off.cell(row=i, column=oc["Score"], value=o.score)
        ws_off.cell(row=i, column=oc["Name delta"], value=o.name_delta)
        u = ws_off.cell(row=i, column=oc["URL"], value=o.url)
        u.hyperlink, u.font = o.url, LINK_FONT
        ws_off.cell(
            row=i,
            column=oc["Fetched"],
            value=(r.fetched_at.get(o.market) or fetched_default)[:19].replace("T", " "),
        )
        for name, f in o_f.items():
            c = ws_off.cell(row=i, column=oc[name], value=f)
            if name == "Baseline":
                c.number_format = MONEY
            elif name == "Delta vs baseline":
                c.number_format = PCT
    off_last = max(len(offers) + 1, 2)
    if not offers:  # a table needs one data row to be valid
        ws_off.cell(row=2, column=1, value="(no offers found)")
    t_off = _table(ws_off, "tblOffers", f"A1:{L(len(o_cols))}{off_last}")
    if offers:
        _calc_columns(ws_off, t_off, {k: ws_off.cell(row=2, column=oc[k]).value[1:] for k in o_f})
    ws_off.freeze_panes = "B2"
    ws_off.auto_filter.ref = None

    # --------------------------------------------------------- Prices (formula grid + heat map)
    p_head = (
        ["Product", "Category", "Our price"]
        + markets
        + [
            "Baseline",
            "Markets matched",
            "Market min",
            "Market median",
            "Delta ours vs median",
            "Cheapest market",
        ]
    )
    _header(ws_prices, 1, p_head, [52, 16, 14] + [14] * nm + [14, 9, 14, 14, 12, 14])
    m0, m1 = 4, 3 + nm  # first / last market column
    bcol, ncol, mincol, medcol, dcol, chcol = (m1 + k for k in range(1, 7))
    assert medcol == prices_median_col

    def R(row):
        return f"{L(m0)}{row}:{L(m1)}{row}"

    for i, t in enumerate(r.targets, 2):
        key = f"$A{i}"
        ws_prices.cell(row=i, column=1, value=f"=INDEX(tblProducts[Product],{i - 1})")
        ws_prices.cell(row=i, column=2, value=f"=INDEX(tblProducts[Category],{i - 1})")
        c = ws_prices.cell(
            row=i,
            column=3,
            value=f'=IF(INDEX(tblProducts[Our price],{i - 1})="","",'
            f"INDEX(tblProducts[Our price],{i - 1}))",
        )
        c.number_format = MONEY
        for j in range(nm):
            col = L(m0 + j)
            c = ws_prices.cell(
                row=i,
                column=m0 + j,
                value=(
                    f'=IF(COUNTIFS(tblOffers[Product],{key},tblOffers[Market],{col}$1)=0,"",'
                    f"_xlfn.MINIFS(tblOffers[Price],tblOffers[Product],{key},tblOffers[Market],{col}$1))"
                ),
            )
            c.number_format = MONEY
        ws_prices.cell(
            row=i,
            column=bcol,
            value=(
                f'=IFERROR(INDEX(tblProducts[Baseline],MATCH({key},tblProducts[Product],0)),"")'
            ),
        ).number_format = MONEY
        ws_prices.cell(row=i, column=ncol, value=f"=COUNT({R(i)})")
        ws_prices.cell(
            row=i, column=mincol, value=f'=IF(COUNT({R(i)})=0,"",MIN({R(i)}))'
        ).number_format = MONEY
        ws_prices.cell(
            row=i, column=medcol, value=f'=IF(COUNT({R(i)})=0,"",MEDIAN({R(i)}))'
        ).number_format = MONEY
        ws_prices.cell(
            row=i,
            column=dcol,
            value=(
                f"=IF(AND(ISNUMBER($C{i}),ISNUMBER({L(medcol)}{i})),IF({L(medcol)}{i}>0,"
                f'$C{i}/{L(medcol)}{i}-1,""),"")'
            ),
        ).number_format = PCT
        ws_prices.cell(
            row=i,
            column=chcol,
            value=(
                f'=IF(COUNT({R(i)})=0,"",INDEX(${L(m0)}$1:${L(m1)}$1,MATCH(MIN({R(i)}),{R(i)},0)))'
            ),
        )
    last = n + 1
    ws_prices.freeze_panes = "D2"
    ws_prices.auto_filter.ref = f"A1:{L(chcol)}{max(last, 2)}"
    # heat map: rules compare each cell with ITS ROW's baseline and read thresholds from Settings
    grid = f"{L(m0)}2:{L(m1)}{max(last, 2)}"
    c0, B = f"{L(m0)}2", f"${L(bcol)}2"
    ok = f"AND(ISNUMBER({c0}),N({B})>0,"
    ws_prices.conditional_formatting.add(
        grid,
        FormulaRule(
            formula=[f"AND(ISNUMBER({c0}),{c0}=MIN(${L(m0)}2:${L(m1)}2))"],
            font=Font(bold=True),
            border=Border(*(Side(style="medium", color="1F3864"),) * 4),
        ),
    )
    rules = [
        (f"{ok}{c0}<={B}*(1-cfg_sat))", HEAT["cheap3"]),
        (f"{ok}{c0}<={B}*(1-cfg_mid))", HEAT["cheap2"]),
        (f"{ok}{c0}<{B}*(1-cfg_par))", HEAT["cheap1"]),
        (f"{ok}{c0}>={B}*(1+cfg_sat))", HEAT["dear3"]),
        (f"{ok}{c0}>={B}*(1+cfg_mid))", HEAT["dear2"]),
        (f"{ok}{c0}>{B}*(1+cfg_par))", HEAT["dear1"]),
    ]
    for formula, colour in rules:
        ws_prices.conditional_formatting.add(
            grid,
            FormulaRule(
                formula=[formula],
                fill=PatternFill("solid", bgColor=colour, fgColor=colour),
                stopIfTrue=True,
            ),
        )

    # --------------------------------------------------------- Heatmap (graded colour scale)
    _header(ws_heat, 1, ["Product"] + markets, [52] + [14] * nm)
    for i in range(2, last + 1):
        ws_heat.cell(row=i, column=1, value=f"=Prices!A{i}")
        for j in range(nm):
            pcell = f"Prices!{L(m0 + j)}{i}"
            ws_heat.cell(
                row=i,
                column=2 + j,
                value=(
                    f'=IF(AND(ISNUMBER({pcell}),N(Prices!${L(bcol)}{i})>0),{pcell}/Prices!${L(bcol)}{i}-1,"")'
                ),
            ).number_format = PCT
    if n:
        ws_heat.conditional_formatting.add(
            f"B2:{L(1 + nm)}{last}",
            ColorScaleRule(
                start_type="formula",
                start_value="-cfg_sat",
                start_color=HEAT["cheap3"],
                mid_type="num",
                mid_value=0,
                mid_color="FFFFFF",
                end_type="formula",
                end_value="cfg_sat",
                end_color=HEAT["dear3"],
            ),
        )
    ws_heat.freeze_panes = "B2"
    ws_heat["A" + str(last + 2)] = (
        "Each cell: (market price - baseline) / baseline. Red = the market is cheaper "
        "than the baseline, green = dearer. Thresholds: Settings."
    )
    ws_heat["A" + str(last + 2)].font = Font(italic=True, color="808080")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ Markets
    mk_cols = [
        "Market",
        "Offers",
        "Products covered",
        "Average delta",
        "Share cheaper than baseline",
        "Cheapest wins",
        "Catalogue fetched",
        "Note",
    ]
    _header(ws_mkt, 1, mk_cols, [14, 9, 16, 14, 18, 12, 24, 70])
    dgrid = f"Heatmap!$B$2:${L(1 + nm)}${max(last, 2)}"
    hhead = f"Heatmap!$B$1:${L(1 + nm)}$1"
    pgrid = f"Prices!${L(m0)}$2:${L(m1)}${max(last, 2)}"
    phead = f"Prices!${L(m0)}$1:${L(m1)}$1"
    pmin = f"Prices!${L(mincol)}$2:${L(mincol)}${max(last, 2)}"
    for i, m in enumerate(markets, 2):
        mref = tr("tblMarkets", "Market")
        ws_mkt.cell(row=i, column=1, value=m)
        ws_mkt.cell(row=i, column=2, value=f"=COUNTIFS(tblOffers[Market],{mref})")
        ws_mkt.cell(
            row=i,
            column=3,
            value=(
                f"=SUMPRODUCT((tblOffers[Market]={mref})/COUNTIFS(tblOffers[Product],tblOffers[Product],"
                f'tblOffers[Market],tblOffers[Market]&""))'
            )
            if offers
            else 0,
        )
        col = f"INDEX({dgrid},0,MATCH({mref},{hhead},0))"
        ws_mkt.cell(row=i, column=4, value=f'=IFERROR(AVERAGE({col}),"")').number_format = PCT
        ws_mkt.cell(
            row=i, column=5, value=f'=IFERROR(COUNTIF({col},"<0")/COUNT({col}),"")'
        ).number_format = "0%"
        ws_mkt.cell(
            row=i,
            column=6,
            value=(
                f"=SUMPRODUCT(--(INDEX({pgrid},0,MATCH({mref},{phead},0))={pmin}),"
                f"--ISNUMBER({pmin}))"
            ),
        )
        ws_mkt.cell(row=i, column=7, value=(r.fetched_at.get(m) or "")[:19].replace("T", " "))
        ws_mkt.cell(
            row=i,
            column=8,
            value=r.notes.get(m) or ("" if m in r.fetched_at else "no data collected"),
        )
    mk_last = nm + 1
    _table(ws_mkt, "tblMarkets", f"A1:{L(len(mk_cols))}{mk_last}")
    ws_mkt.freeze_panes = "B2"
    if nm and offers:
        ch = BarChart()
        ch.type, ch.style = "bar", 2
        ch.title = "Average market price vs baseline, by market"
        ch.title.overlay = False
        ch.add_data(Reference(ws_mkt, min_col=4, min_row=1, max_row=mk_last), titles_from_data=True)
        ch.set_categories(Reference(ws_mkt, min_col=1, min_row=2, max_row=mk_last))
        # openpyxl 3.1 hides both axes unless told otherwise; labels go "low" so a negative bar
        # does not run over its own market name
        ch.x_axis.delete = False
        ch.y_axis.delete = False
        ch.x_axis.tickLblPos = "low"
        ch.x_axis.scaling.orientation = "maxMin"  # first market on top, as in the table
        ch.y_axis.number_format = "0%"
        ch.y_axis.majorGridlines.spPr = None
        ch.legend = None
        serie = ch.series[0]
        serie.graphicalProperties.solidFill = NAVY
        serie.invertIfNegative = False
        ch.height, ch.width = 9, 18
        ws_mkt.add_chart(ch, f"A{mk_last + 3}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ Links (as-fetched snapshot)
    _header(ws_links, 1, ["Product", "Category", "Our price"] + markets, [52, 16, 14] + [14] * nm)
    cheapest = {}
    for o in offers:
        k = (o.target, o.market)
        if k not in cheapest or o.price < cheapest[k].price:
            cheapest[k] = o
    for i, t in enumerate(r.targets, 2):
        ws_links.cell(row=i, column=1, value=t.name)
        ws_links.cell(row=i, column=2, value=t.category or "")
        up = _uzs(t, r.usd_rate)
        ws_links.cell(row=i, column=3, value=up).number_format = MONEY
        for j, m in enumerate(markets):
            o = cheapest.get((t.name, m))
            if o:
                c = ws_links.cell(row=i, column=4 + j, value=o.price)
                c.hyperlink, c.font, c.number_format = o.url, LINK_FONT, MONEY
    ws_links.freeze_panes = "D2"

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ Notes
    found = {o.target for o in offers}
    stale = ", ".join(f"{m} {t[:16].replace('T', ' ')}" for m, t in sorted(r.fetched_at.items()))
    nodata = ", ".join(
        m for m in markets if m not in r.fetched_at or not any(o.market == m for o in offers)
    )
    rows = [
        ("Run", r.run_at.replace("T", " ")),
        ("Products", f"{n} researched, {len(found)} with at least one offer."),
        ("Catalogue freshness", stale or "n/a"),
        ("Markets with no offers", nodata or "none"),
        (
            "A market cell",
            "The cheapest accepted offer of that market for that product (Prices = formulas over "
            "tblOffers; Links = as-fetched snapshot with hyperlinks). Every other offer stays "
            "in Offers.",
        ),
        (
            "Market median",
            "Median of one representative (cheapest) price per market, so a marketplace with many "
            "sellers does not outvote a single-shop retailer.",
        ),
        (
            "Baseline",
            "Settings!cfg_baseline: 'Our price' (default) or 'Market median'. A product with no "
            "price of "
            "ours uses the median.",
        ),
        (
            "Delta vs baseline",
            "(market price - baseline) / baseline. Negative = the market is cheaper than the "
            "baseline. 'Delta ours vs median' on Prices is the other way round: "
            "(our price - median) / median, positive = we are dearer.",
        ),
        ("Empty cell", "No match found. Never a price of zero."),
        (
            "Identity rule",
            "Brand, model, RAM and storage must match; colour is not identity except on iPhones. "
            "Phones accept at score >= 30, other categories at >= 25 (brand + model tokens "
            "both ways).",
        ),
        (
            "Name delta",
            "Words present in one name but not the other. Not an error by itself: read it before "
            "quoting a row.",
        ),
        (
            "Heat map",
            "Conditional formatting over Prices and Heatmap, thresholds from Settings. "
            "It follows edits "
            "and re-sorts; nothing is painted by hand.",
        ),
        (
            "Formulas",
            "MINIFS / MAXIFS need Excel 2019 / Microsoft 365 (or LibreOffice). Pivot "
            "refresh: Data > "
            "Refresh All; PivotTables read tblOffers, which grows when you add rows.",
        ),
        (
            "Currency",
            "Prices in UZS."
            + (
                f" USD inputs converted at {r.usd_rate:,.2f} UZS ({r.usd_rate_date})."
                if r.usd_rate
                else ""
            ),
        ),
    ]
    ws_notes.column_dimensions["A"].width = 26
    ws_notes.column_dimensions["B"].width = 120
    for i, (k, v) in enumerate(rows, 1):
        ws_notes.cell(row=i, column=1, value=k).font = Font(bold=True)
        ws_notes.cell(row=i, column=2, value=v).alignment = Alignment(
            wrap_text=True, vertical="top"
        )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ Start
    ws_start.column_dimensions["A"].width = 24
    ws_start.column_dimensions["B"].width = 100
    ws_start["A1"] = r.title
    ws_start["A1"].font = Font(bold=True, size=16, color=NAVY)
    ws_start["A2"] = (
        f"{n} products x {nm} markets, {len(offers)} offers. Built {datetime.now():%Y-%m-%d %H:%M}."
    )
    guide = [
        (
            "Prices",
            "Heat map: cheapest price per market, coloured against your baseline. "
            "Bold border = cheapest in row.",
        ),
        ("Heatmap", "The same grid as percentage differences, graded colour scale."),
        (
            "Offers",
            "The database: every offer. Pivot source. Add or delete rows; formulas fill "
            "and the grid follows.",
        ),
        (
            "Products",
            "Your products. Yellow cells (Our price, Category) are inputs: change them and "
            "everything follows.",
        ),
        ("Markets", "Scoreboard per market, with a chart."),
        ("Links", "As-fetched prices, each one a hyperlink to its offer."),
        ("Settings", "Heat-map thresholds and the baseline."),
        ("Notes", "Definitions, freshness, what an empty cell means."),
    ]
    for i, (sheet, text) in enumerate(guide, 4):
        c = ws_start.cell(row=i, column=1, value=sheet)
        c.hyperlink = Hyperlink(ref=c.coordinate, location=f"'{sheet}'!A1", display=sheet)
        c.font = LINK_FONT
        ws_start.cell(row=i, column=2, value=text)
    ws_start.cell(row=13, column=1, value="PivotTables").font = Font(bold=True)
    ws_start.cell(
        row=13,
        column=2,
        value=(
            "Sheets named 'Pivot ...' (if present) read tblOffers. Data > Refresh All "
            "after you change anything. To build your own: Insert > PivotTable > "
            "Table/Range = tblOffers."
        ),
    )

    wb.save(path)
    return {
        "markets": markets,
        "n_products": n,
        "n_offers": len(offers),
        "offers_cols": o_cols,
        "path": path,
        "prices_cols": {"first_market": m0, "last_market": m1, "baseline": bcol},
    }



================================================
FILE: narxnoma-crawler/narxnoma/research/excel/__init__.py
================================================
"""PivotTables and Excel-side verification for research workbooks.

Two ways to get real PivotTables into the file `narxnoma.research.workbook` writes:

    pivots.py        Excel COM (Windows + desktop Excel): flat pivots, slicers, a pivot chart,
                     and -- when this Excel has Power Pivot -- a Data Model with DAX measures.
    ooxml_pivot.py   no Excel at all: the pivot parts are written into the .xlsx package
                     directly and Excel builds them when the file is opened.

Run either AFTER the workbook is built and never re-save the result through openpyxl: it drops
slicers and the Data Model silently.
"""



================================================
FILE: narxnoma-crawler/narxnoma/research/excel/ooxml_pivot.py
================================================
"""PivotTables with no Excel: write the pivot parts into the .xlsx package and let Excel build them.

openpyxl cannot create a PivotTable (it can only carry one through), and COM needs desktop Excel.
This writes the minimum a PivotTable needs -- a cache definition whose source is the Excel Table
`tblOffers`, an empty records part, and one pivot-table definition per spec -- and marks the cache
`refreshOnLoad`, so Excel fills the shared items, the totals and the cells itself on first open.
That is the same trick libraries such as EPPlus use. What it does NOT give you: slicers, pivot
charts, the Data Model, number formats beyond the built-in ones (the COM path has all of them).

Run on a workbook built with `build_workbook(..., pivot_placeholders=True)`: the empty
'Pivot ...' sheets it creates are where these land. Never re-save the result through openpyxl.
"""

import posixpath
import re
import zipfile
from dataclasses import dataclass
from xml.sax.saxutils import escape

NS_MAIN = "http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/spreadsheetml/2006/main"
NS_R = "http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships"
REL = "http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships"
CT_DEF = "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.pivotCacheDefinition+xml"
CT_REC = "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.pivotCacheRecords+xml"
CT_TBL = "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.pivotTable+xml"

_AXIS_FIELD = (
    '<pivotField axis="{axis}" showAll="0">'
    '<items count="1"><item t="default"/></items></pivotField>'
)

# built-in number formats: 3 = #,##0   10 = 0.00%   (custom formats would need a styles.xml edit)
NUMFMT_INT, NUMFMT_PCT = 3, 10


@dataclass
class PivotSpec:
    name: str  # pivot table name, e.g. "pvtCategoryMarket"
    sheet: str  # placeholder sheet it lands on
    anchor: str  # top-left cell of the pivot, e.g. "A5"
    row_field: str  # a column of tblOffers
    col_field: str
    data_field: str
    function: str  # sum | count | average | min | max
    caption: str
    numfmt: int = NUMFMT_INT


DEFAULT_SPECS = (
    PivotSpec(
        "pvtCategoryMarket",
        "Pivot by category",
        "A5",
        "Category",
        "Market",
        "Delta vs baseline",
        "average",
        "Avg delta vs baseline",
        NUMFMT_PCT,
    ),
    PivotSpec(
        "pvtPriceMatrix",
        "Pivot price matrix",
        "A5",
        "Product",
        "Market",
        "Price",
        "min",
        "Cheapest price",
        NUMFMT_INT,
    ),
    PivotSpec(
        "pvtMarketBands",
        "Pivot market bands",
        "A5",
        "Market",
        "Band",
        "Product",
        "count",
        "Offers",
        NUMFMT_INT,
    ),
)


def _cache_xml(fields: list[str]) -> str:
    cf = "".join(
        f'<cacheField name="{escape(f)}" numFmtId="0"><sharedItems/></cacheField>' for f in fields
    )
    return (
        f'<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>\n'
        f'<pivotCacheDefinition xmlns="{NS_MAIN}" xmlns:r="{NS_R}" r:id="rId1" refreshOnLoad="1" '
        f'refreshedBy="narxnoma" refreshedDate="45000" createdVersion="3" refreshedVersion="3" '
        f'minRefreshableVersion="3" recordCount="0"><cacheSource type="worksheet">'
        f'<worksheetSource name="tblOffers"/></cacheSource><cacheFields count="{len(fields)}">{cf}'
        f"</cacheFields></pivotCacheDefinition>"
    )


def _records_xml() -> str:
    return (
        f'<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>\n'
        f'<pivotCacheRecords xmlns="{NS_MAIN}" xmlns:r="{NS_R}" count="0"/>'
    )


def _table_xml(spec: PivotSpec, fields: list[str], cache_id: int) -> str:
    ri, ci, di = (fields.index(f) for f in (spec.row_field, spec.col_field, spec.data_field))
    pf = []
    for i, _ in enumerate(fields):
        if i == ri:
            pf.append(_AXIS_FIELD.format(axis="axisRow"))
        elif i == ci:
            pf.append(_AXIS_FIELD.format(axis="axisCol"))
        elif i == di:
            pf.append('<pivotField dataField="1" showAll="0"/>')
        else:
            pf.append('<pivotField showAll="0"/>')
    col = re.match(r"([A-Z]+)(\d+)", spec.anchor)
    ref = f"{spec.anchor}:{col.group(1)}{int(col.group(2)) + 2}"  # a stub: Excel resizes on refresh
    return (
        f'<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>\n'
        f'<pivotTableDefinition xmlns="{NS_MAIN}" name="{escape(spec.name)}" cacheId="{cache_id}" '
        f'dataCaption="Values" updatedVersion="3" minRefreshableVersion="3" createdVersion="3" '
        f'useAutoFormatting="1" itemPrintTitles="1" indent="0" outline="1" outlineData="1" '
        f'multipleFieldFilters="0"><location ref="{ref}" firstHeaderRow="1" firstDataRow="2" '
        f'firstDataCol="1"/>'
        f'<pivotFields count="{len(fields)}">{"".join(pf)}</pivotFields>'
        f'<rowFields count="1"><field x="{ri}"/></rowFields>'
        f'<colFields count="1"><field x="{ci}"/></colFields>'
        f'<dataFields count="1"><dataField name="{escape(spec.caption)}" fld="{di}" '
        f'subtotal="{spec.function}" '
        f'baseField="0" baseItem="0" numFmtId="{spec.numfmt}"/></dataFields>'
        f'<pivotTableStyleInfo name="PivotStyleMedium2" showRowHeaders="1" showColHeaders="1" '
        f'showRowStripes="1" showColStripes="0" showLastColumn="1"/></pivotTableDefinition>'
    )


def _rels_xml(rels: list[tuple[str, str, str]]) -> str:
    body = "".join(f'<Relationship Id="{i}" Type="{t}" Target="{tg}"/>' for i, t, tg in rels)
    return (
        '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>\n'
        f'<Relationships xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships">{body}</Relationships>'
    )


def _next_rid(rels_xml: str) -> str:
    ids = [int(m) for m in re.findall(r'Id="rId(\d+)"', rels_xml)]
    return f"rId{max(ids, default=0) + 1}"


def inject_pivots(src: str, dst: str, offers_fields: list[str], specs=DEFAULT_SPECS) -> list[str]:
    """Copy `src` to `dst` with the pivot parts added. Returns the names of the pivots written."""
    zin = zipfile.ZipFile(src)
    parts = {n: zin.read(n) for n in zin.namelist()}
    zin.close()

    wbx = parts["xl/workbook.xml"].decode("utf-8")
    wbr = parts["xl/_rels/workbook.xml.rels"].decode("utf-8")
    ctx = parts["[Content_Types].xml"].decode("utf-8")
    sheet_target = {}
    for m in re.finditer(r'<sheet\b[^>]*?name="([^"]+)"[^>]*?r:id="([^"]+)"', wbx):
        t = re.search(
            rf'<Relationship\b[^>]*?Id="{m.group(2)}"[^>]*?Target="([^"]+)"', wbr
        ) or re.search(rf'<Relationship\b[^>]*?Target="([^"]+)"[^>]*?Id="{m.group(2)}"', wbr)
        sheet_target[m.group(1)] = posixpath.normpath(
            posixpath.join("xl", t.group(1).lstrip("/").removeprefix("xl/"))
        )
    missing = [s.sheet for s in specs if s.sheet not in sheet_target]
    if missing:
        raise ValueError(
            f"placeholder sheet(s) missing: {missing}; build with pivot_placeholders=True"
        )

    cache_id = 1
    parts["xl/pivotCache/pivotCacheDefinition1.xml"] = _cache_xml(offers_fields).encode("utf-8")
    parts["xl/pivotCache/pivotCacheRecords1.xml"] = _records_xml().encode("utf-8")
    parts["xl/pivotCache/_rels/pivotCacheDefinition1.xml.rels"] = _rels_xml(
        [("rId1", f"{REL}/pivotCacheRecords", "pivotCacheRecords1.xml")]
    ).encode("utf-8")
    overrides = [
        ("/xl/pivotCache/pivotCacheDefinition1.xml", CT_DEF),
        ("/xl/pivotCache/pivotCacheRecords1.xml", CT_REC),
    ]

    rid = _next_rid(wbr)
    wbr = wbr.replace(
        "</Relationships>",
        f'<Relationship Id="{rid}" Type="{REL}/pivotCacheDefinition" '
        f'Target="pivotCache/pivotCacheDefinition1.xml"/></Relationships>',
    )
    pc_xml = (
        f'<pivotCaches><pivotCache xmlns:r="{NS_R}" cacheId="{cache_id}" r:id="{rid}"/>'
        "</pivotCaches>"
    )
    if "<pivotCaches" in wbx:
        raise ValueError("workbook already has pivot caches")
    if re.search(r"<calcPr\b[^>]*/>", wbx):
        wbx = re.sub(r"(<calcPr\b[^>]*/>)", r"\1" + pc_xml, wbx, count=1)
    else:
        wbx = wbx.replace("</workbook>", pc_xml + "</workbook>")

    written = []
    for n, spec in enumerate(specs, 1):
        tpath = f"xl/pivotTables/pivotTable{n}.xml"
        parts[tpath] = _table_xml(spec, offers_fields, cache_id).encode("utf-8")
        parts[f"xl/pivotTables/_rels/pivotTable{n}.xml.rels"] = _rels_xml(
            [("rId1", f"{REL}/pivotCacheDefinition", "../pivotCache/pivotCacheDefinition1.xml")]
        ).encode("utf-8")
        overrides.append((f"/{tpath}", CT_TBL))
        sheet_part = sheet_target[spec.sheet]
        rels_part = posixpath.join(
            posixpath.dirname(sheet_part), "_rels", posixpath.basename(sheet_part) + ".rels"
        )
        cur = parts.get(rels_part, _rels_xml([]).encode("utf-8")).decode("utf-8")
        srid = _next_rid(cur)
        cur = cur.replace(
            "</Relationships>",
            f'<Relationship Id="{srid}" Type="{REL}/pivotTable" '
            f'Target="../pivotTables/pivotTable{n}.xml"/></Relationships>',
        )
        parts[rels_part] = cur.encode("utf-8")
        written.append(spec.name)

    ctx = ctx.replace(
        "</Types>",
        "".join(f'<Override PartName="{p}" ContentType="{c}"/>' for p, c in overrides) + "</Types>",
    )
    parts["xl/workbook.xml"] = wbx.encode("utf-8")
    parts["xl/_rels/workbook.xml.rels"] = wbr.encode("utf-8")
    parts["[Content_Types].xml"] = ctx.encode("utf-8")

    with zipfile.ZipFile(dst, "w", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zout:
        zout.writestr("[Content_Types].xml", parts.pop("[Content_Types].xml"))  # must be first
        for name, data in parts.items():
            zout.writestr(name, data)
    return written



================================================
FILE: narxnoma-crawler/narxnoma/research/excel/pivots.ps1
================================================
<#
  Add real PivotTables, slicers and a pivot chart to a research workbook through Excel COM.

      powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File pivots.ps1 -Path book.xlsx [-Out out.xlsx] [-Model auto|yes|no]

  Input is a workbook written by narxnoma.research.workbook (Excel Tables tblOffers / tblProducts /
  tblMarkets). Output lines: PIVOT, SLICER, CHART, MODEL, WARN, RESULT.

  Three flat PivotTables read `tblOffers` through ONE shared pivot cache (so one slicer drives all
  three):
      Pivot by category       Category x Market   average delta vs baseline, colour scale, + pivot chart
      Pivot price matrix      Product  x Market   cheapest price
      Pivot market bands      Market   x Band     number of offers per heat band
  Slicers: Category, Market, Band.

  Data Model (Power Pivot): with -Model auto the script PROBES whether the Excel here has it. If yes it
  loads the three tables, relates Offers[Product] -> Products[Product] and Offers[Market] ->
  Markets[Market], adds DAX measures and one model-based PivotTable. If not (or on any failure) it
  prints "MODEL unavailable: <reason>" and the workbook keeps the three flat pivots -- the flat tables
  are the fallback, nothing is lost. -Model yes fails loudly instead of falling back; -Model no skips it.

  The same hard-won rules as the BI cube tooling: every COM call retries on 0x800AC472 ("busy");
  number formats are set on the data field, not painted on cells; optional steps never abort the build.
  Re-saving the result through openpyxl would silently drop the slicers: do not.
#>
param(
  [Parameter(Mandatory = $true)][string]$Path,
  [string]$Out = "",
  [ValidateSet("auto", "yes", "no")][string]$Model = "auto"
)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'

function Invoke-Com([scriptblock]$Block, [string]$What) {
  for ($attempt = 1; $attempt -le 30; $attempt++) {
    try { return & $Block } catch {
      if ($_.Exception.Message -notmatch '800AC472|RPC_E_|0x8001010A') { throw "$What : $($_.Exception.Message)" }
      Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 400
    }
  }
  throw "Excel is not responding: $What"
}
function Try-Step([string]$What, [scriptblock]$Block) {
  try { Invoke-Com $Block $What | Out-Null } catch { Write-Output "WARN $What not done: $($_.Exception.Message)" }
}

$file = (Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $Path).Path
$target = if ($Out) { $Out } else { $file }
$xl = New-Object -ComObject Excel.Application
$xl.Visible = $false; $xl.DisplayAlerts = $false
try {
  $wb = Invoke-Com { $xl.Workbooks.Open($file) } "open"
  Invoke-Com { $xl.CalculateFull() } "calculate" | Out-Null          # the pivot must see values, not unevaluated formulas
  for ($w = 0; $w -lt 120; $w++) { if ((Invoke-Com { $xl.CalculationState } "state") -eq 0) { break }; Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 300 }

  function New-Sheet([string]$name, [string]$title, [string]$note) {
    $s = Invoke-Com { $wb.Worksheets.Add() } "sheet $name"
    Invoke-Com { $s.Name = $name } "name $name" | Out-Null
    Invoke-Com { $s.Move($wb.Worksheets.Item("Offers")) } "move $name" | Out-Null      # pivots sit before the data sheets
    Invoke-Com { $s.Range("A1").Value2 = $title; $s.Range("A1").Font.Bold = $true; $s.Range("A1").Font.Size = 14 } "title" | Out-Null
    Invoke-Com { $s.Range("A2").Value2 = $note; $s.Range("A2").Font.Italic = $true; $s.Range("A2").Font.Color = 8421504 } "note" | Out-Null
    return $s
  }
  function Finish-Pivot($pt, [string]$rowLabel = "", [string]$colLabel = "") {
    # Header and grand-total captions otherwise follow the Excel UI language ("Row Labels", "Grand
    # Total", or their translations): the same workbook would read differently on every machine.
    if ($rowLabel) { Try-Step "row header caption" { $pt.CompactLayoutRowHeader = $rowLabel } }
    if ($colLabel) { Try-Step "column header caption" { $pt.CompactLayoutColumnHeader = $colLabel } }
    Try-Step "grand total caption" { $pt.GrandTotalName = "Total" }
    Try-Step "pivot style" { $pt.TableStyle2 = "PivotStyleMedium2"; $pt.ShowTableStyleRowStripes = $true }
    Try-Step "keep column widths" { $pt.HasAutoFormat = $false }
    Try-Step "preserve formatting" { $pt.PreserveFormatting = $true }
  }

  $pc = Invoke-Com { $wb.PivotCaches().Create(1, "tblOffers") } "pivot cache"          # 1 = xlDatabase
  Try-Step "refresh on open" { $pc.RefreshOnFileOpen = $true }
  $pivots = @()

  # ---- 1: Category x Market, average delta vs baseline ----
  $ws1 = New-Sheet "Pivot by category" "Average market price vs baseline, by category and market" `
        "Negative (red) = the market is cheaper than your baseline. Slicers on the right filter all three pivot sheets. Data > Refresh All after edits."
  $pt1 = Invoke-Com { $pc.CreatePivotTable($ws1.Range("A5"), "pvtCategoryMarket") } "pivot 1"
  Invoke-Com { $pt1.PivotFields("Category").Orientation = 1 } "rows" | Out-Null
  Invoke-Com { $pt1.PivotFields("Market").Orientation = 2 } "columns" | Out-Null
  $df = Invoke-Com { $pt1.AddDataField($pt1.PivotFields("Delta vs baseline"), "Avg delta vs baseline", -4106) } "data field"   # xlAverage
  Invoke-Com { $df.NumberFormat = "+0.0%;-0.0%;0.0%" } "format" | Out-Null
  Finish-Pivot $pt1 "Category" "Market"
  Try-Step "colour scale on the pivot" {
    $cs = $pt1.DataBodyRange.FormatConditions.AddColorScale(3)
    $cs.ScopeType = 2                                                  # xlDataFieldScope: the scale follows the pivot as it changes
    $cs.ColorScaleCriteria.Item(1).Type = 0; $cs.ColorScaleCriteria.Item(1).Value = -0.2; $cs.ColorScaleCriteria.Item(1).FormatColor.Color = 7039480    # F8696B
    $cs.ColorScaleCriteria.Item(2).Type = 0; $cs.ColorScaleCriteria.Item(2).Value = 0;    $cs.ColorScaleCriteria.Item(2).FormatColor.Color = 16777215
    $cs.ColorScaleCriteria.Item(3).Type = 0; $cs.ColorScaleCriteria.Item(3).Value = 0.2;  $cs.ColorScaleCriteria.Item(3).FormatColor.Color = 8109667    # 63BE7B
  }
  $pivots += $pt1
  Write-Output "PIVOT pvtCategoryMarket on '$($ws1.Name)' $($pt1.TableRange1.Address($false,$false))"

  # ---- 2: Product x Market, cheapest price ----
  $ws2 = New-Sheet "Pivot price matrix" "Cheapest price by product and market" "The same numbers as Prices, as a pivot: drag Category / Band / Availability into rows, columns or filters."
  $pt2 = Invoke-Com { $pc.CreatePivotTable($ws2.Range("A5"), "pvtPriceMatrix") } "pivot 2"
  Invoke-Com { $pt2.PivotFields("Product").Orientation = 1 } "rows" | Out-Null
  Invoke-Com { $pt2.PivotFields("Market").Orientation = 2 } "columns" | Out-Null
  $df2 = Invoke-Com { $pt2.AddDataField($pt2.PivotFields("Price"), "Cheapest price", -4139) } "data field"                              # xlMin
  Invoke-Com { $df2.NumberFormat = "#,##0" } "format" | Out-Null
  Finish-Pivot $pt2 "Product" "Market"
  $pivots += $pt2
  Write-Output "PIVOT pvtPriceMatrix on '$($ws2.Name)' $($pt2.TableRange1.Address($false,$false))"

  # ---- 3: Market x Band, offer counts ----
  $ws3 = New-Sheet "Pivot market bands" "Offers per heat band, by market" "How many offers sit in each band against your baseline (band edges: Settings)."
  $pt3 = Invoke-Com { $pc.CreatePivotTable($ws3.Range("A5"), "pvtMarketBands") } "pivot 3"
  Invoke-Com { $pt3.PivotFields("Market").Orientation = 1 } "rows" | Out-Null
  Invoke-Com { $pt3.PivotFields("Band").Orientation = 2 } "columns" | Out-Null
  $df3 = Invoke-Com { $pt3.AddDataField($pt3.PivotFields("Product"), "Offers", -4112) } "data field"                                  # xlCount
  Invoke-Com { $df3.NumberFormat = "#,##0" } "format" | Out-Null
  Finish-Pivot $pt3 "Market" "Band"
  $pivots += $pt3
  Write-Output "PIVOT pvtMarketBands on '$($ws3.Name)' $($pt3.TableRange1.Address($false,$false))"

  # ---- slicers: one cache, so each slicer drives every pivot ----
  foreach ($f in @("Category", "Market", "Band")) {
    Try-Step "slicer $f" {
      $sc = $wb.SlicerCaches.Add2($pt1, $f)
      for ($k = 1; $k -lt $pivots.Count; $k++) { $sc.PivotTables.AddPivotTable($pivots[$k]) }
      # right of the pivot AND of the chart below it (the chart is 620 pt wide, starting at 10)
      $left = [Math]::Max($pt1.TableRange1.Left + $pt1.TableRange1.Width + 25, 10 + 620 + 25)
      $top = 60 + ([array]::IndexOf(@("Category", "Market", "Band"), $f)) * 190
      [void]$sc.Slicers.Add($ws1, [Type]::Missing, "slc$f", $f, $top, $left, 170, 175)
    }
    Write-Output "SLICER $f"
  }

  # ---- pivot chart under pivot 1 ----
  Try-Step "pivot chart" {
    $shape = $ws1.Shapes.AddChart2(201, 51, 10, $pt1.TableRange1.Top + $pt1.TableRange1.Height + 25, 620, 300)
    $shape.Chart.SetSourceData($pt1.TableRange1)
    $shape.Chart.HasTitle = $true; $shape.Chart.ChartTitle.Text = "Average market price vs baseline"
    $shape.Chart.HasLegend = $true; $shape.Chart.Legend.Position = -4107              # xlLegendPositionBottom: one colour per market
    $shape.Chart.ShowAllFieldButtons = $false                                         # the slicers do the filtering
  }
  Write-Output "CHART on '$($ws1.Name)'"

  # ---- Data Model, probed ----
  $modelState = "skipped (-Model no)"
  if ($Model -ne "no") {
    try {
      $m = Invoke-Com { $wb.Model } "wb.Model"                       # property absent / null when Power Pivot is not part of this Excel
      if ($null -eq $m) { throw "workbook.Model is not available in this Excel" }
      foreach ($t in @("tblOffers", "tblProducts", "tblMarkets")) {
        $cn = "WorksheetConnection_$t"
        [void](Invoke-Com { $wb.Connections.Add2($cn, "", "WORKSHEET;$file", $t, 7, $true, $false) } "model table $t")   # 7 = xlCmdExcel, True = add to the Data Model
      }
      $tabs = @{}
      foreach ($mt in $m.ModelTables) { $tabs[$mt.Name] = $mt }
      $o = $tabs["tblOffers"]; $p = $tabs["tblProducts"]; $mk = $tabs["tblMarkets"]
      if (-not ($o -and $p -and $mk)) { throw "the three tables did not all reach the model (found: $($tabs.Keys -join ', '))" }
      function Col($tab, $name) { foreach ($c in $tab.ModelTableColumns) { if ($c.Name -eq $name) { return $c } }; throw "no column $name in $($tab.Name)" }
      [void]$m.ModelRelationships.Add((Col $o "Product"), (Col $p "Product"))
      [void]$m.ModelRelationships.Add((Col $o "Market"), (Col $mk "Market"))
      $pctFmt = $m.ModelFormatPercentageNumber(1)
      $numFmt = $m.ModelFormatDecimalNumber(1, 0)
      [void]$m.ModelMeasures.Add("Offers count", $o, "COUNTROWS(tblOffers)", $m.ModelFormatWholeNumber(1), "Number of offers")
      [void]$m.ModelMeasures.Add("Median price", $o, "MEDIAN(tblOffers[Price])", $m.ModelFormatDecimalNumber(1, 0), "Median offer price")
      [void]$m.ModelMeasures.Add("Cheapest price", $o, "MIN(tblOffers[Price])", $m.ModelFormatDecimalNumber(1, 0), "Lowest offer price")
      [void]$m.ModelMeasures.Add("Avg delta", $o, "AVERAGE(tblOffers[Delta vs baseline])", $pctFmt, "Average delta vs baseline")
      [void]$m.ModelMeasures.Add("Share cheaper than baseline", $o, "DIVIDE(CALCULATE(COUNTROWS(tblOffers), tblOffers[Delta vs baseline] < 0), COUNTROWS(tblOffers))", $pctFmt, "Share of offers cheaper than the baseline")
      $ws4 = New-Sheet "Pivot model (DAX)" "Data Model pivot: Markets table x DAX measures" "Offers, Products and Markets are related in the Data Model; the measures are DAX. Filter by a Products column to cross tables."
      $mcn = $wb.Connections.Item("ThisWorkbookDataModel")
      $pcm = Invoke-Com { $wb.PivotCaches().Create(2, $mcn) } "model cache"      # 2 = xlExternal
      $ptm = Invoke-Com { $pcm.CreatePivotTable($ws4.Range("A5"), "pvtModel") } "model pivot"
      Invoke-Com { $ptm.CubeFields.Item("[tblMarkets].[Market]").Orientation = 1 } "model rows" | Out-Null
      foreach ($ms in @("Offers count", "Cheapest price", "Median price", "Avg delta", "Share cheaper than baseline")) {
        Invoke-Com { $ptm.CubeFields.Item("[Measures].[$ms]").Orientation = 4 } "measure $ms" | Out-Null
      }
      Finish-Pivot $ptm "Market"
      $modelState = "ok (3 tables, 2 relationships, 5 DAX measures)"
    } catch {
      if ($Model -eq "yes") { throw }
      $modelState = "unavailable: $($_.Exception.Message)"
    }
  }
  Write-Output "MODEL $modelState"

  Try-Step "select first sheet" { $wb.Worksheets.Item("Start").Activate() }
  Invoke-Com { $xl.CalculateFull() } "final calculate" | Out-Null
  if ($Out) { Invoke-Com { $wb.SaveAs($target, 51) } "save as" | Out-Null } else { Invoke-Com { $wb.Save() } "save" | Out-Null }
  Invoke-Com { $wb.Close($false) } "close" | Out-Null
  Write-Output "RESULT ok $target"
} finally {
  try { $xl.Quit() } catch { }
  [void][Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal]::ReleaseComObject($xl)
}

FILE: narxnoma-crawler/narxnoma/research/excel/pivots.py

"""Drive Excel through COM (PowerShell) to add PivotTables and to verify a workbook."""

import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent


class ExcelUnavailable(RuntimeError):
    pass


@dataclass
class ComResult:
    ok: bool
    lines: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    @property
    def warnings(self) -> list[str]:
        return [ln for ln in self.lines if ln.startswith("WARN")]

    @property
    def model(self) -> str:
        return next((ln[6:] for ln in self.lines if ln.startswith("MODEL ")), "")

    def __str__(self) -> str:
        return "\n".join(self.lines)


def excel_available() -> bool:
    """True when this is Windows, PowerShell is present and the Excel COM class is registered.
    Checked through the registry: starting Excel just to ask costs seconds and may pop a dialog."""
    if sys.platform != "win32" or not shutil.which("powershell"):
        return False
    probe = subprocess.run(
        [
            "powershell",
            "-NoProfile",
            "-Command",
            "if (Test-Path 'Registry::HKEY_CLASSES_ROOT\\Excel.Application') {'yes'} else {'no'}",
        ],
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        timeout=60,
    )
    return probe.stdout.strip() == "yes"


def _run(script: str, args: list[str], timeout: int) -> ComResult:
    if not excel_available():
        raise ExcelUnavailable(
            "desktop Excel is not available here (needs Windows + Excel); "
            "use the raw-OOXML pivots or open the workbook and Insert > PivotTable"
        )
    cmd = [
        "powershell",
        "-NoProfile",
        "-ExecutionPolicy",
        "Bypass",
        "-File",
        str(HERE / script),
        *args,
    ]
    p = subprocess.run(
        cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace", timeout=timeout
    )
    lines = [ln.rstrip() for ln in (p.stdout or "").splitlines() if ln.strip()]
    if p.returncode != 0:
        lines.append(
            "FAILED " + (p.stderr or "").strip().splitlines()[0] if p.stderr.strip() else "FAILED"
        )
    return ComResult(ok=p.returncode == 0, lines=lines)


def add_pivots(
    path: str | Path, out: str | Path | None = None, model: str = "auto", timeout: int = 600
) -> ComResult:
    """Flat PivotTables + slicers + chart; Data Model when `model` allows and Excel has it."""
    args = ["-Path", str(Path(path).resolve()), "-Model", model]
    if out:
        args += ["-Out", str(Path(out).resolve())]
    return _run("pivots.ps1", args, timeout)


def verify(path: str | Path, probe: str = "", save: bool = False, timeout: int = 300) -> ComResult:
    """Let Excel open and recalculate the workbook, then report formula errors, tables, pivots,
    slicers and conditional-format rule counts. `save=True` also bakes the calculated values in,
    so viewers that cannot calculate (previews, pandas) see numbers."""
    args = ["-Path", str(Path(path).resolve())]
    if probe:
        args += ["-Probe", probe]
    if save:
        args.append("-Save")
    return _run("verify.ps1", args, timeout)


def error_count(result: ComResult) -> int | None:
    for ln in result.lines:
        if ln.startswith("ERRORS_TOTAL"):
            return int(ln.split()[1])
    return None



================================================
FILE: narxnoma-crawler/narxnoma/research/excel/verify.ps1
================================================
<#
  Open a research workbook in real Excel, recalculate it, and report what Excel itself sees.

      powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File verify.ps1 -Path book.xlsx [-Save] [-Probe "Prices!D2,Prices!E2"]

  Why this exists: openpyxl writes formulas but cannot evaluate them, so a typo (#NAME?, #REF!, a
  function that needs a newer Excel) is invisible until a person opens the file. This is the same
  trick as recalculating through COM in the BI cube tooling: let Excel do it and read back.

  Reports, one line each:  OPEN, ERRORS (count + first cells per sheet), TABLES, PIVOTS, SLICERS,
  CF (conditional-format rules per sheet), PROBE (value and fill colour of the cells you name).
  -Save writes the recalculated values back, so viewers that do not calculate (previews, pandas)
  see numbers instead of blanks. Never run this against a workbook a person has open.

  Excel automation sometimes answers 0x800AC472 ("busy"); every call retries.
#>
param(
  [Parameter(Mandatory = $true)][string]$Path,
  [switch]$Save,
  [string]$Probe = ""
)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'

function Invoke-Com([scriptblock]$Block, [string]$What) {
  for ($attempt = 1; $attempt -le 25; $attempt++) {
    try { return & $Block } catch {
      if ($_.Exception.Message -notmatch '800AC472|RPC_E_|0x8001010A') { throw "$What : $($_.Exception.Message)" }
      Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 400
    }
  }
  throw "Excel is not responding: $What"
}

$file = (Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $Path).Path
$xl = New-Object -ComObject Excel.Application
$xl.Visible = $false; $xl.DisplayAlerts = $false
try {
  $wb = Invoke-Com { $xl.Workbooks.Open($file) } "open"
  Invoke-Com { $xl.CalculateFull() } "calculate" | Out-Null
  for ($w = 0; $w -lt 120; $w++) { if ((Invoke-Com { $xl.CalculationState } "state") -eq 0) { break }; Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 300 }
  "OPEN  $($wb.Name)  Excel $($xl.Version)  sheets=$($wb.Worksheets.Count)"
  $totalErr = 0
  foreach ($ws in $wb.Worksheets) {
    $errs = 0; $first = @()
    try {
      $cells = Invoke-Com { $ws.UsedRange.SpecialCells(-4123, 16) } "errors in $($ws.Name)"   # xlCellTypeFormulas, xlErrors
      foreach ($c in $cells.Cells) { $errs++; if ($first.Count -lt 3) { $first += "$($c.Address($false,$false))=$($c.Text)" } }
    } catch { }                                                                                # no matching cells -> exception
    $totalErr += $errs
    $cf = Invoke-Com { $ws.Cells.FormatConditions.Count } "cf count"
    $lo = Invoke-Com { $ws.ListObjects.Count } "tables"
    $pv = Invoke-Com { $ws.PivotTables().Count } "pivots"
    "SHEET $($ws.Name): errors=$errs $($first -join ' ') | tables=$lo pivots=$pv cf_rules=$cf"
  }
  $slicers = 0
  try { $slicers = $wb.SlicerCaches.Count } catch { }
  "SLICERS $slicers"
  "ERRORS_TOTAL $totalErr"
  if ($Probe) {
    foreach ($ref in $Probe.Split(",")) {
      $sheet, $addr = $ref.Trim().Split("!")
      $c = Invoke-Com { $wb.Worksheets.Item($sheet).Range($addr) } "probe $ref"
      $rgb = Invoke-Com { $c.DisplayFormat.Interior.Color } "colour $ref"
      $hex = "{0:X2}{1:X2}{2:X2}" -f ($rgb -band 255), (($rgb -shr 8) -band 255), (($rgb -shr 16) -band 255)
      "PROBE $ref text=[$($c.Text)] fill=$hex bold=$($c.DisplayFormat.Font.Bold)"
    }
  }
  if ($Save) { Invoke-Com { $wb.Save() } "save" | Out-Null; "SAVED" }
  Invoke-Com { $wb.Close($false) } "close" | Out-Null
} finally {
  try { $xl.Quit() } catch { }
  [void][Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal]::ReleaseComObject($xl)
}
