        if _is_storage_range(figures):
            saw_range = True
            continue
        return _collapse(match.group(0))
    if saw_range:
        # The single-figure fallback below would otherwise pick one HALF of
        # the range it just refused -- "128/256GB" yields "256GB" -- which is
        # the same coin toss with the evidence hidden.
        return ""
    match = _MEMORY_SINGLE_RE.search(title)
    return _collapse(match.group(0)) if match else ""


# "Smartfon Samsung Galaxy A16", "Смартфон Samsung ...", "Telefon ...".
# Harmless while the whole head is the name (it is what the retailer wrote),
# but fatal once the head is cut down to a prefix: it is a product-type noun
# carrying no identity, and keeping it in place of the brand is what made
# ("Smartfon Samsung Galaxy A16, ...", "A16 8/256") parse to
# "Smartfon A16 Qora".
_LEAD_NOUN_RE = re.compile(r"^(?:smartfon|смартфон|telefon|телефон)\w*\b[\s:,-]*", re.I)

_SUBTOKEN_RE = re.compile(r"[^0-9A-Za-zЀ-ӿ]+")


def _prefix_for_model(head: str, model_v: str) -> str:
    """The brand-bearing part of the card head, to sit in front of a Модель.

    A bare Модель value ("17 Pro Max 256 GB", "A16 8/256") carries no brand
    of its own, so the head has to supply it. The head is kept only UP TO
    the first word the Модель value already states, so
    "IPhone 17 Pro/Pro Max" + "17 Pro Max 256 GB" yields "IPhone" while
    "Samsung Galaxy A16" + "A16 8/256" keeps "Samsung Galaxy".

    Truncating rather than filtering is what stops a card lending a SKU the
    model it is NOT. uzum bundles siblings on one card -- "IPhone 17 Pro /
    Max 256/512 GB" carries both the Pro and the Pro Max -- and dropping
    only the words the Модель repeats left "Max" standing in the prefix of
    the plain "17 Pro 256" SKU, which then keyed as a Pro Max. Once a head
    word belongs to the model, everything after it is model territory and
    the Модель value owns it: it is the per-SKU field, and the head is the
    shared one.
    """
    model_tokens = {t for t in _SUBTOKEN_RE.split(model_v.lower()) if t}
    kept = []
    for word in _LEAD_NOUN_RE.sub("", head).split():
        subtokens = [t for t in _SUBTOKEN_RE.split(word.lower()) if t]
        if subtokens and all(t in model_tokens for t in subtokens):
            break
        kept.append(word)
    return " ".join(kept)


def _build_name_raw(card_title: str | None, model_v: str, mem_v: str, color_v: str) -> str:
    """Best-effort faithful name -- and the clustering stage's ONLY handle on
    this product's identity. See the module docstring for the three rules
    and the bug each of them fixes.
    """
    full_title = (card_title or "").strip()
    head = full_title.split(",")[0].strip()

    if model_v:
        # The SKU's own Модель wins outright and is never rewritten.
        spec = model_v
        prefix = _prefix_for_model(head, model_v)
        if mem_v or _states_memory(model_v):
            # ...but the card head in front of it must not smuggle the
            # card's overall storage RANGE in beside it (section 5b).
            prefix = _strip_memory(prefix)
    else:
        prefix = ""
        spec = _strip_memory(head) if mem_v else head

    name = _collapse(" ".join(p for p in (prefix, spec, mem_v) if p))

    # Last resort, and ONLY when this SKU stated no memory of its own. The
    # card title past the first comma is shared across every SKU the card
    # bundles, so reaching into it for a capacity the SKU already supplied
    # replaces a specific figure with a range -- see _states_memory.
    if not _states_memory(name) and not (mem_v or _states_memory(model_v)):
        recovered = _memory_from_title(full_title)
        if recovered:
            name = _collapse(f"{name} {recovered}")
    return _collapse(f"{name} {color_v}" if color_v else name)


def _items(payload: dict) -> list[dict]:
    return (((payload or {}).get("data") or {}).get("makeSearch") or {}).get("items") or []


def _card_image(card: dict) -> str | None:
    """The card's first photo, or None.

    uzum was the only source publishing no picture at all -- 5,461 offers,
    the largest source in the catalogue, every one of them imageless, because
    `photos` was simply never asked for. The site's placeholder-heavy look
    traced back to this one missing field.

    Two things about the field are not guessable and were read off the live
    schema: `photos.link` REQUIRES a `trans:` argument (`Transformation!`), so
    a bare `link { high }` is a validation error and a 400, not a null; and
    PhotoLink's `high`/`low` are plain URL strings, not objects. PRODUCT_540
    is a card-sized render -- ORIGINAL exists but is a full-resolution
    original, which is the wrong thing to hotlink into a grid.

    The photo belongs to the CARD, and a card is finer-grained than a
    product: makeSearch returns ONE CARD PER COLOUR, so a single productId
    comes back as several cards (16 of 72 items in the recorded fixture) whose
    skuLists differ only in storage tier. The picture is therefore accurate to
    the colour, not merely to the model -- which matters here, because colour
    is part of a phone's identity in this catalogue and a card showing the
    black handset under the green variant would be a substitution.
    """
    photos = card.get("photos")
    if not isinstance(photos, list):
        return None
    for photo in photos:
        if not isinstance(photo, dict):
            continue
        link = photo.get("link")
        url = link.get("high") if isinstance(link, dict) else None
        if isinstance(url, str) and url.startswith("http"):
            return url
    return None


def _parse_sku(card: dict, sku: dict) -> Listing | None:
    if not isinstance(sku, dict):
        return None
    sku_id = sku.get("id")
    if sku_id is None:
        return None
    product_id = card.get("productId")
    if product_id is None:
        return None

    price = coerce_price(sku.get("sellPrice"))  # never fullPrice -- see module docstring
    chars = _characteristics(sku)
    name_raw = _build_name_raw(card.get("title"), chars["model"], chars["memory"], chars["color"])

    try:
        amount = int(sku.get("availableAmount") or 0)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        amount = 0
    # Deliberate inversion of docs/parsers/uzum.md section 4 -- see module
    # docstring. Kept, not dropped; OUT_OF_STOCK rather than discarded.
    availability = "IN_STOCK" if amount > 0 else "OUT_OF_STOCK"

    return try_build_listing(
        store_slug="uzum",
        source_key=str(sku_id),
        name_raw=name_raw,
        price=price,
        url=PRODUCT_URL.format(product_id=product_id, sku_id=sku_id),
        availability=availability,
        image_url=_card_image(card),
        raw={
            "card_title": card.get("title"),      # untouched, for re-derivation downstream
            "category_id": (card.get("category") or {}).get("id"),
            "product_id": product_id,
            "sku_id": sku_id,
            "characteristic_values": sku.get("characteristicValues") or [],
            "available_amount": sku.get("availableAmount"),
            "full_price": sku.get("fullPrice"),
        },
    )


def parse(payload: dict, category: str = "smartphones") -> list[Listing]:
    """Turn one raw makeSearch response into Listings. Pure function, no I/O.

    Unlike idea/beemarket, category filtering happens inside parse() itself
    rather than as a separate crawl-policy step in sweep() -- search() hits
    the same noisy relevance endpoint sweep() does, so both need the filter
    and there is no separate category-scoped endpoint to lean on instead.

    Handles the `errors`-key failure mode (docs/parsers/uzum.md section 2)
    by returning [] rather than raising -- the live fetch path
    (UzumSource._fetch_payload) is what raises on this, so a broken sweep
    call is loud instead of silently looking like an empty brand.
    """
    if (payload or {}).get("errors"):
        logger.warning("uzum: graphql payload carries an errors key, returning no listings: %s",
                        payload.get("errors"))
        return []

    ids = CATEGORY_IDS.get(category)
    if ids is None:
        raise ValueError(f"unknown category {category!r}")
    listings: list[Listing] = []
    for item in _items(payload):
        card = (item or {}).get("catalogCard") or {}
        category_id = (card.get("category") or {}).get("id")
        if category_id not in ids:
            continue
        for sku in card.get("skuList") or []:
            lst = _parse_sku(card, sku)
            if lst is not None:
                if lst.category != category:
                    lst = replace(lst, category=category)
                listings.append(lst)
    return listings


def _search_variables(text: str, offset: int) -> dict:
    return {
        "q": {
            "text": text,
            "showAdultContent": "TRUE",
            "filters": [],
            "sort": "BY_RELEVANCE_DESC",
            "pagination": {"offset": offset, "limit": PAGE_LIMIT},
        }
    }


class UzumSource:
    slug = "uzum"
    can_sweep = True

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._session = None

    def _get_session(self):
        if self._session is None:
            self._session = polite_session(
                referer="https://uzum.uz/",
                origin="https://uzum.uz",
                extra_headers={
                    "Accept-Language": "ru-RU",
                    "apollographql-client-name": "web-customers",  # required
                    "apollographql-client-version": "1.63.2",
                    "city-id": "1",
                },
            )
        return self._session

    def _post_page(self, text: str, offset: int) -> dict:
        session = self._get_session()
        token, iid = _get_token()
        return post_json(
            session,
            GRAPHQL_URL,
            headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}", "x-iid": iid},
            json={"query": SEARCH_QUERY, "variables": _search_variables(text, offset)},
        )

    def _fetch_payload(self, text: str, offset: int) -> dict:
        """POST one makeSearch page, raising on the errors-key failure mode
        (docs/parsers/uzum.md section 2) rather than treating it as an
        empty/end-of-results page -- see module docstring for why.

        A 401 re-mints the token once and retries. The cached token is only
        refreshed when its locally decoded `exp` passes, which cannot see a
        server-side revocation: without this, a token revoked in the middle
        of a brand sweep propagated an HTTPError all the way out of sweep()
        and ended the run, even though minting a new one takes one request.
        A second 401 is a genuine auth failure and is left to raise.
        """
        try:
            payload = self._post_page(text, offset)
        except requests.HTTPError as exc:
            if getattr(exc.response, "status_code", None) != 401:
                raise
            logger.warning(
                "uzum: 401 for %r@%d -- re-minting the token and retrying once", text, offset
            )
            _invalidate_token()
            payload = self._post_page(text, offset)
        errors = (payload or {}).get("errors")
        if errors:
            raise UzumGraphQLError(f"uzum graphql errors for {text!r}@{offset}: {errors!r}")
        return payload

    def _walk(self, text: str, category: str = "smartphones") -> Iterator[Listing]:
        def fetch(offset):
            return self._fetch_payload(text, offset)

        def following(payload, offset):
            # Two end signals: the offset cap (docs say results run out
            # before it for most brands) and the items running out -- uzum
            # has no last_page, so an empty payload IS its end marker
            # (base.paginate's rule: the client whose end signal is an
            # empty payload expresses that in its own next_page).
            nxt = offset + PAGE_LIMIT
            if nxt > OFFSET_CAP:
                return None
            return nxt if _items(payload) else None

        yield from paginate(fetch, lambda payload: parse(payload, category), following, first_page=0)

    def sweep(self, category: str = "smartphones") -> Iterator[Listing]:
        """Every item uzum returns across this category's query frontier,
        paginating each until its items run out or offset hits OFFSET_CAP
        (see module docstring for why offset can't just run unbounded).
        Deduplicated by source_key (SKU id) across queries, since the same SKU
        can surface under more than one query.

        Phones sweep by BRAND; everything else sweeps by the product NOUN.
        Using the brand frontier for a category left uzum holding zero air
        conditioners, fridges, washers and ovens: the brands that sell white
        goods here sell no phones and so were never queried, and for Samsung,
        which sells both, relevance ranks phones first and pagination caps at
        offset 1000 so a fridge never surfaced. Measured live, "konditsioner"
        returns 72 of 72 in-category while "artel" returns remote controls,
        vacuum filters and electric kettles. See sources/brand_queries.py.
        """
        seen: set[str] = set()
        for query in queries_for(category):
            # Per-page retries live in base.paginate() via _walk.
            for listing in self._walk(query, category):
                if listing.source_key in seen:
                    continue
                seen.add(listing.source_key)
                yield listing

    def search(self, query: str) -> Iterator[Listing]:
        payload = self._fetch_payload(query, 0)
        yield from parse(payload)



================================================
FILE: skills/adding-a-narxnoma-source/SKILL.md
================================================
---
name: adding-a-narxnoma-source
description: Use when adding a new retailer or marketplace to the narxnoma engine, when a site seems unscrapable because its HTML is an empty shell, or when deciding whether a store is worth adding.
---

# Adding a source

## Overview

A source is one file implementing `Source` (`narxnoma/sources/base.py`),
one recorded fixture, one test module, one doc, and four registrations.
Everything the client claims must be asserted against the fixture; the doc
is written so the client could be rebuilt from it alone.

## 1. Find the durable path

```dot
digraph find_path {
    "Open site, DevTools > Network > Fetch/XHR, type a search" [shape=box];
    "Response with prices?" [shape=diamond];
    "Copy as cURL; that JSON API is the source" [shape=box];
    "Site server-rendered? (view-source shows priced cards)" [shape=diamond];
    "Parse HTML from machine attributes (data-*, ids), never prose" [shape=box];
    "Read the JS bundles for an API host (radius: new.api.)" [shape=box];
    "Open site, DevTools > Network > Fetch/XHR, type a search" -> "Response with prices?";
    "Response with prices?" -> "Copy as cURL; that JSON API is the source" [label="yes"];
    "Response with prices?" -> "Site server-rendered? (view-source shows priced cards)" [label="no"];
    "Site server-rendered? (view-source shows priced cards)" -> "Parse HTML from machine attributes (data-*, ids), never prose" [label="yes"];
    "Site server-rendered? (view-source shows priced cards)" -> "Read the JS bundles for an API host (radius: new.api.)" [label="no"];
}
```

Check `robots.txt` first. If it disallows the browse surface, you need
written permission from the site (see skill `fetch-glotr`) or you stop.

Prefer, in order: category/catalogue endpoint (olcha, beemarket) → broad
query walk (texnomart) → brand-query list (idea, alifshop, share
`brand_queries.py`) → search-only with `can_sweep = False` (mediapark).

## 2. Write the client

```python
# narxnoma/sources/<slug>.py
API_URL = "https://..."
PRODUCT_URL = "https://.../{id}"          # verified in a browser, not guessed

def _parse_item(item: dict) -> Listing | None:
    price = coerce_price(item.get("<the one current-price field>"))
    return try_build_listing(store_slug="<slug>", source_key=str(item["id"]),
                             name_raw=item["name"], price=price,
                             url=PRODUCT_URL.format(id=item["id"]),
                             image_url=item.get("image"), raw=item)

def parse(payload: dict) -> list[Listing]:     # pure function of one response
    ...

class <Slug>Source:
    slug = "<slug>"
    can_sweep = True
    def sweep(self, category="smartphones") -> Iterator[Listing]:  # uses base.paginate
    def search(self, query) -> Iterator[Listing]:
```

Invariants the base layer enforces or expects:

- `parse()` never filters by category; crawl policy lives in `sweep()`.
- Use `base.paginate(fetch, parse, next_page)`: retries one bad page, raises
  when a page fails outright, and only `next_page` ends the sweep — never
  an empty page.
- Multi-seller site: one listing per seller offer, `source_key` = offer id.
- Variant-level site (radius): one listing per variant, name from the variant.
- Price unit checked against the rendered page; `PRICE_MIN..PRICE_MAX` fence.
- GraphQL: inspect `errors` under HTTP 200.
- HTML: read the product id from two places and drop the card when they disagree.

## 3. Record a fixture and test

Save one real response to `tests/fixtures/sources/<slug>.json|html` (redact
nothing; it is public catalogue data). Tests in `tests/sources/test_<slug>.py`
assert, from the fixture: count parsed, one exact price and URL, the
end-of-pagination signal, and the trap you found. Add a `@pytest.mark.live`
case to `test_contract_live.py` asserting the endpoint shape.

## 4. Register

| where | what |
|---|---|
| `narxnoma/sources/__init__.py` | add to `SOURCES` |
| `narxnoma/pipeline/report.py` | `EXPECTED_MIN_OFFERS["<slug>"]` — measured floor; a source below it is flagged SUSPICIOUS |
| `narxnoma/db/schema.sql` | new migration inserting the `store` row (slug, name, url) |
| `narxnoma/matching/brands.py` | any new brands the source introduces |
| `docs/parsers/<slug>.md` + `README.md` table | canonical notes |
| `skills/fetch-<slug>/SKILL.md` | operational card |

## 5. Measure before calling it done

Run `python -m narxnoma.cli fetch --source <slug>`, open three built URLs
in a browser, compare three prices to the page. Then, with Postgres,
`cycle` and read the coverage report: the number that matters is how many
products got a **second** price, not how many rows arrived (radius: 2.2% of
offers, 4.8% of comparisons).

## Common mistakes

| Mistake | Fix |
|---|---|
| Stop paging on an empty page | Stop on the site's own pager / total count; empty pages are filtered pages |
| Build the slug yourself | Take it verbatim; open it |
| Trust `status_code` | GraphQL and some REST return 200 with an error body |
| One code path divides by 100, another does not | Single price coercion function per client |
| Doc written after the fact | Rebuild the client from the doc; if you cannot, the doc is incomplete |

FILE: skills/fetch-alifshop/SKILL.md
================================================
---
name: fetch-alifshop
description: Use when fetching prices or listings from alifshop.uz, when alifshop prices look 100x too high, or when an alifshop product link 404s.
---

# alifshop.uz — REST POST, multi-seller, price in tiyin

Notes: `docs/parsers/alifshop.md`. Client: `narxnoma-crawler/narxnoma/sources/alifshop.py`.

## Run

```bash
cd narxnoma-crawler   # pip install -e . once
python -m narxnoma.cli fetch --source alifshop --limit 60
python -m narxnoma.cli fetch --source alifshop --query "honor x9d"
```

## Endpoint

```
POST https://gw.alifshop.uz/web/client/search/full-text
Content-Type: application/json
Accept-Language: ru
service-token: service-token-alifshop     # fixed public string, not a secret
Origin: https://alifshop.uz
Referer: https://alifshop.uz/
Body: {"query": "Honor X9d", "page": 0, "size": 20}
```

- `page` is **0-based** (beemarket/texnomart are 1-based).
- `size` capped at **20** server-side; 3 pages per brand is enough for a median.
- No catalogue browse: `sweep()` loops the shared brand list
  (`sources/brand_queries.py`), one search per brand.

## Price — tiyin

`items[].price` is in **tiyin (1/100 UZS)**. Divide by 100 once, in one place
(`_coerce_tiyin_price`). A partial divisor bug yields 100x prices that still
pass plausibility fences on some rows.

## Category

Items carry no category field. `aggregations.category` is a histogram whose
bucket names (`"Смартфоны Samsung"`) the endpoint accepts back as a `category`
filter and honours server-side. Discover the bucket per brand, then re-request
filtered; fall back to unfiltered with a warning if none matches.

## URL

`https://alifshop.uz/moderated-offer/<slug>` — the obvious `/product/<slug>` **404s**.
Use the API's slug verbatim.

## Traps

- Multi-seller: `source_key` is the listing UUID, one row per partner offer.
  Never collapse sellers; price = IQR-trimmed median.
- Query is strict AND; two-shot: colour-stripped first, then verbatim.
- `+` only means `plus` when trailing a word (`Pro+`); `nanoSim+eSim` must not
  mint a `plus` model token.
- Sellers omit RAM: missing RAM on a candidate = unknown, not reject.

Fixture: `tests/fixtures/sources/alifshop.json`. Tests: `tests/sources/test_alifshop*.py`.



================================================
FILE: skills/fetch-asaxiy/SKILL.md
================================================
---
name: fetch-asaxiy
description: Use when fetching prices or listings from asaxiy.uz, when looking for an asaxiy JSON API, when asaxiy titles come back in Uzbek, or when an asaxiy sweep loops re-ingesting the same products.
---

# asaxiy.uz — server-rendered HTML, no JSON API at all

The exception to "call the SPA's API": asaxiy's only `/ajax/*` routes are
login and filter plumbing. Category pages ship a complete, priced product
grid in the initial HTML. Notes: `docs/parsers/asaxiy.md`.
Client: `narxnoma-crawler/narxnoma/sources/asaxiy.py`.

## Run

```bash
cd narxnoma-crawler   # pip install -e . once
python -m narxnoma.cli fetch --source asaxiy --limit 50
python -m narxnoma.cli fetch --source asaxiy --category washers
```

No `search()`.

## Request

```
GET https://asaxiy.uz/ru/product/<category-path>            # page 1
GET https://asaxiy.uz/ru/product/<category-path>/page=<N>   # page N >= 2
Accept: text/html
Accept-Language: ru-RU,ru;q=0.9
```

- Page number is a **path segment**; `?page=2` is ignored.
- Russian by `/ru/` path. `Accept-Language` and the `_language` cookie are
  ignored; `?language=ru` only works via a 301 that `X-Requested-With:
  XMLHttpRequest` suppresses.
- Do **not** send `X-Requested-With`: the lighter fragment drops the
  `totalPages` script, the only end-of-catalogue signal.
- Category paths in `CATEGORY_PATHS`.

## Read machine attributes, not prose

Each card carries a hidden `<span id="product_cart_data_<id>" data-id
data-name data-name-ru data-price data-old-price data-img data-brand-name>`
— the values asaxiy's own cart uses. Read `data-price` (integer UZS, no
separators) and `data-name-ru`. Never parse `6 719 000 so'm`.

## URL — tied to the id, twice

Anchor: `<a href="/ru/product/<slug>" onclick="sendSelectItemEvent(<id>, this)">`.
Require that id to equal the cart block's id; drop the card otherwise. A
markup shift must cost a listing, never mint one with a neighbour's URL.

## Availability

CSS class `js-buy-guest` present = IN_STOCK, absent = OUT_OF_STOCK. Do not read
the buy href (localised: scored every Russian card out-of-stock) or the
`Нет в наличии` text (Russian-only).

## Pagination trap

An out-of-range page **301s to page 1 and serves 24 real products again**.
Stop on `let totalPages = parseInt('6', 10)` from the page's inline script
AND on the first page adding no unseen id.

## Category leaves are broader than the category

A leaf holds neighbours (e.g. freezers under fridges). Filter by the
category noun; see doc §6.

Fixtures: `tests/fixtures/sources/asaxiy_ru.html`, `asaxiy_uz.html`. Tests: `tests/sources/test_asaxiy*.py`.



================================================
FILE: skills/fetch-beemarket/SKILL.md
================================================
---
name: fetch-beemarket
description: Use when fetching prices or listings from market.beeline.uz (BeeMarket), or when a BeeMarket sweep returns thousands of non-phone items.
---

# market.beeline.uz — REST GET, single seller, category endpoint

Notes: `docs/parsers/beemarket.md`. Client: `narxnoma-crawler/narxnoma/sources/beemarket.py`.

## Run

```bash
cd narxnoma-crawler   # pip install -e . once
python -m narxnoma.cli fetch --source beemarket --limit 50
python -m narxnoma.cli fetch --source beemarket --query "Honor X6c"
```

## Endpoints

Sweep — category-scoped, the request the site's own category page makes:

```
GET https://market.beeline.uz/api/web/categories/<slug>/products?page=<1-based>&is_bshop=1
```

Search — generic, honours `q`:

```
GET https://market.beeline.uz/api/web/search?q=<text>&is_bshop=1&page=<1-based>
```

Headers: `Accept-Language: ru`, `Referer: https://market.beeline.uz/`. No auth.
Both return the doubly-nested `data.data[]` list with `data.last_page`. Stop
on `last_page`.

Category slugs: `CATEGORY_SLUGS` in the client (`smartfony` = smartphones).
Adding `category=`/`category_id=` to the generic search is silently ignored —
only the separate category endpoint narrows.

## Price

`actual_price` → `discount_price` → `price` (struck-through list; last resort).
Integer UZS.

## URL

`https://market.beeline.uz/<category.slug>/<slug>` — take `slug` verbatim;
BeeMarket writes a literal `.` as `point` inside slugs.

## Traps

- Search is strict AND on product names: `q=smartfon` returns one accessory.
  Broad query, strict match.
- `SIM+eSIM` in names: only a trailing `+` is `plus`.
- Colour sits mid-name (`Honor X9d, Midnight Black, 12/256 GB`): parse by
  token, not position.
- Accessories carry no storage; the storage rule drops them.

Fixture: `tests/fixtures/sources/beemarket.json`. Tests: `tests/sources/test_beemarket*.py`.



================================================
FILE: skills/fetch-glotr/SKILL.md
================================================
---
name: fetch-glotr
description: Use when asked to fetch listings from glotr.uz (robots.txt forbids it; only with the site's written permission), when every glotr picture belongs to the neighbouring product, or when a glotr sweep ingests far more rows than the category holds.
---

# glotr.uz — server-rendered classifieds board

**Access first.** glotr's `robots.txt` disallows the browse surface
(`/search`, `/*?catid=`, `/*?page=`, `Crawl-delay: 10`) for every user agent
and names `ClaudeBot`/`GPTBot`/`CCBot` with `Disallow: /`. The original
operator swept it under an **explicit written grant from glotr**. That grant
does not travel with this code. Do not run this client against glotr.uz
without your own permission from them, and do not copy the approach to
another site whose robots.txt says the same.

Notes: `docs/parsers/glotr.md`. Client: `narxnoma-crawler/narxnoma/sources/glotr.py`.

## Run (with permission)

```bash
cd narxnoma-crawler   # pip install -e . once
python -m narxnoma.cli fetch --source glotr --category ovens --limit 50
```

No `search()`.

## Request

```
GET https://glotr.uz/<category-path>/            # page 1
GET https://glotr.uz/<category-path>/?page=N
Accept-Language: ru-RU,ru;q=0.9
```

No auth. Yii-rendered, UTF-8. 56 cards per page. `--category` maps to the
path via `CATEGORY_PATHS`:

| `--category` | path |
|---|---|
| smartphones | `telefoni-i-plansheti` |
| televisions | `televizori` |
| laptops | `noutbuki` |
| acs | `konditsioneri` |
| fridges | `xolodilniki` |
| washers | `stiralnie-mashini` |
| ovens | `duxovie-shkafi` |

robots.txt states `Crawl-delay: 10`. Honour it even under a grant:
`NARXNOMA_MIN_DELAY=10 NARXNOMA_MAX_DELAY=12`.

## A classifieds board, not a shop

Sellers post into any category they like. Every category page is noisy:
filter with **positive keeps** (title must carry the category noun) plus an
accessory drop — never a denylist alone. See doc §6.

## Card — the photos come before the name

```
<div class='product-card '>
  header:  <a href="/<slug>-p-<id>/"> <img data-src=... alt=...>   (photos)
  content: <a href='/<slug>-p-<id>/' class='product-card__link'>name</a>
           <span class='text-stock text-success'>В наличии</span>
           <div class="price-retail proposal-price"> 2 610 800 <span>сум</span></div>
```

Cut cards at `<div class='product-card '` (encloses both halves). Read the
`-p-<id>` **twice** — photo anchor and name anchor — and drop the card when
they disagree. Cutting at the name anchor gave 55/55 listings their
neighbour's picture while every count stayed green.

## Price and availability

Price text `7 579 000` — strip spaces, `coerce_price`. Cards with no price
("цена по запросу") are dropped with a warning.
`text-success` = IN_STOCK; `text-warning` (под заказ), `text-secondary`
(уточнить), absent = UNKNOWN. **Nothing maps to OUT_OF_STOCK**: sellers
delist rather than mark.

## Pagination trap — filler past the end

Beyond the last page glotr serves **21 unrelated products, a different set
every time**, under the category's own heading. "No new ids" never fires; a
240-oven category ingested 2,739. Stop when the pager's `<li class="next">`
has no `<a>` (last page is `next disabled`; past the end there is no
`pagination-list` at all). The pager is windowed (page 1 links 2–5 only);
never size the catalogue from it. Keep no-new-ids as a backstop.

Fixtures: `tests/fixtures/sources/glotr*.html`. Tests: `tests/sources/test_glotr.py`.

FILE: skills/fetch-idea/SKILL.md
================================================
---
name: fetch-idea
description: Use when fetching prices or listings from idea.uz, when an idea search answers 404, or when idea names arrive without a brand.
---

# idea.uz — REST GET, single seller, brand queries, no pagination

Notes: `docs/parsers/idea.md`. Client: `narxnoma-crawler/narxnoma/sources/idea.py`.

## Run

```bash
cd narxnoma-crawler   # pip install -e . once
python -m narxnoma.cli fetch --source idea --limit 50
python -m narxnoma.cli fetch --source idea --query "Galaxy A36"
```

## Endpoint

```
GET https://api.idea.uz/api/v2/search?search=<text>&quantity=100&type[]=shops&type[]=products
Accept-Language: uz
Origin: https://idea.uz
Referer: https://idea.uz/
```

- `type[]` is a repeated parameter (`params={"type[]": ["shops", "products"]}`).
- `quantity` honoured up to ~100. **No pagination.**
- No catalogue browse: `sweep()` runs the shared brand list and dedupes by `id`.
- **A barren query answers HTTP 404, not an empty list.** Tolerate 404 per
  query and continue; still raise on 5xx and connection errors.

## Name and price

- `name` is **brandless** (`Galaxy S25 Ultra 12/256 Titanium Blue`); use
  `title_name`, which carries the brand, falling back to `name`.
- `current_price`, integer UZS.

## Category

Responses mix appliances into every query. `category_id` is the only usable
signal (`55` = smartphones; map in `CATEGORY_IDS`); textual category fields are
null. Filter in `sweep()`, not `parse()`.

## URL

`products[].url` is a complete absolute URL. Use verbatim.

## Traps

- Part numbers in names (`SM-A376EZADSOZ`) read as model tokens: skip mixed
  alphanumeric tokens of length ≥ 5.
- `Accept-Language: uz` is what the site sends; `ru` changes names. Keep stable.

Fixture: `tests/fixtures/sources/idea.json`. Tests: `tests/sources/test_idea*.py`.

FILE: skills/fetch-mediapark/SKILL.md
================================================
---
name: fetch-mediapark
description: Use when fetching prices from mediapark.uz, when asked to sweep mediapark's catalogue, or when a mediapark product slug disagrees with its name.
---

# mediapark.uz — autocomplete only, no sweep

Notes: `docs/parsers/mediapark.md`. Client: `narxnoma-crawler/narxnoma/sources/mediapark.py`.

## Run

```bash
cd narxnoma-crawler   # pip install -e . once
python -m narxnoma.cli fetch --source mediapark --query "Samsung Galaxy A17 6/128 Black"
python -m narxnoma.cli fetch --source mediapark --query "iPhone 16 128"     # Apple: capacity, no GB
```

`fetch --source mediapark` without `--query` exits 1: `can_sweep = False`.
There is no catalogue endpoint and no pagination; a sweep is structurally
impossible. Reach it per known variant (`pipeline/backfill.py` does this after
clustering).

## Endpoint

```
GET https://api.v2.mediapark.uz/v1/diginetica/autocomplete?st=<text>&lang=ru
Accept-Language: ru
Origin: https://mediapark.uz
Referer: https://mediapark.uz/
x-device-id: <any value>      # must be present, any string
x-session-id: <any value>
```

Parameter is `st`, not `q`. Returns top ~5–10 ranked hits in one call.

## Price and URL

- `products[].price` (string) — run through `coerce_price`. `oldPrice` unused.
- `linkUrl` verbatim, prefixed with `https://mediapark.uz`. Route resolves by
  the trailing numeric id; **the slug lies** (`...8-128-gb...` on a 256 GB
  phone). Never parse or rebuild it.

## Query — opposite of strict-AND sites

Ranking engine, not a filter: keep the query near-verbatim, including the
`6/128` form and colour, so the exact variant ranks into the short list.
Strip only the `Смартфон` prefix and `GB`. Aggressive stripping measurably
worsens recall here.

## Traps

- `SAMSUNG SM-A075F A07`: skip mixed alphanumeric tokens ≥ 5 chars; never
  require the word `galaxy`.
- No per-item category; the `categories` key is suggestion links, unrelated to items.
- Headless browsers hit bot detection; JSON is the only route.

Fixture: `tests/fixtures/sources/mediapark.json`. Tests: `tests/sources/test_mediapark.py`.

FILE: skills/fetch-olcha/SKILL.md
================================================
---
name: fetch-olcha
description: Use when fetching prices or listings from olcha.uz, when an olcha product link 404s, or when olcha returns only 20 items per page.
---

# olcha.uz — REST GET, full-catalogue pull

Notes: `docs/parsers/olcha.md`. Client: `narxnoma-crawler/narxnoma/sources/olcha.py`.

## Run

```bash
cd narxnoma-crawler   # pip install -e . once
python -m narxnoma.cli fetch --source olcha --limit 100
python -m narxnoma.cli fetch --source olcha --category televisions
python -m narxnoma.cli fetch --source olcha --query "redmi note 14"   # sweeps smartphones, filters client-side
```

`--category` defaults to `smartphones`. `--query` has no endpoint of its own:
it sweeps the category (~23 pages, 15–30 s) and keeps names containing the
query; `--limit` counts kept rows, so it cannot drop the match.

## Endpoint

```
GET https://mobile.olcha.uz/api/v2/products?page=<n>&per_page=20&category=<alias>
Accept-Language: ru
clientmodel: web            # required
Origin: https://olcha.uz
Referer: https://olcha.uz/
```

- Host is `mobile.` — the app API. No auth.
- `per_page` capped at **20** server-side; ~460 phones is ~23 pages. Do not
  try to shortcut it.
- Category aliases in `CATEGORY_ALIASES` (`telefony`, `televizory`, `noutbuki`,
  `konditsionery`, `kholodilniki-i-morozilnye-kamery-1`, `stiralnye-mashiny`,
  `plity-gazovye-i-elektricheskie`). Leaf aliases come from `/api/v2/categories`.

Why catalogue, not search: olcha's search caps results per query; the
category pull is complete and unranked. Use this pattern on any site whose
category holds hundreds, not thousands.

## Price

`discount_price` (int) falling back to `total_price` (string) — both through
`coerce_price`. Integer UZS.

## URL

`https://olcha.uz/ru/product/view/<alias>` — `/ru/product/<alias>` without
`/view/` **404s**. Use the API's `alias` verbatim.

## Traps

- Accessories slip into the category; the storage rule drops them.
- Names can be `Samsung Galaxy A37 5G 8 128 GB Awesome Gray Green` — a match
  that looks like a colour mismatch may be right. Read the raw name before
  "fixing" a matcher.

Fixture: `tests/fixtures/sources/olcha.json`. Tests: `tests/sources/test_olcha*.py`.

FILE: skills/fetch-openshop/SKILL.md
================================================
---
name: fetch-openshop
description: Use when fetching prices or listings from openshop.uz (web.openshop.uz API), when an openshop sweep stops at ~2,400 products or 48 per page, when an openshop link opens the API or an Uzbek page, or when matching a whole assortment against openshop's catalogue.
---

# openshop.uz — REST POST, full-catalogue sweep

Single-seller shop; the whole catalogue (~5,700 products) is ~120 requests.
Notes: `docs/parsers/openshop.md`. Client: `narxnoma-crawler/narxnoma/sources/openshop.py`.

## Run

```bash
cd narxnoma-crawler   # pip install -e . once
python -m narxnoma.cli fetch --source openshop --limit 50
python -m narxnoma.cli fetch --source openshop --category ovens
python -m narxnoma.cli fetch --source openshop --query "honor x9d"    # fuzzy: pick by name_raw
```

Whole catalogue, every product type, no price fence (Python):

```python
from narxnoma.sources.openshop import OpenshopSource
items = list(OpenshopSource().iter_catalogue())   # raw dicts, walked per top category
```

## Endpoint

```
POST https://web.openshop.uz/api/v1/shop/product/get_products
     ?category_id=<top id>&page=<n>&per_page=48&sort_by=latest     # filters in the QUERY STRING
Accept-Language: ru
language: ru                 # custom header; without it names are Uzbek
Origin: https://openshop.uz
Referer: https://openshop.uz/
```

- Response `{data:[items], meta:{current_page,last_page,per_page,total}}`. Stop on
  `meta.last_page`, never on an empty page.
- Also accepts `subcategory_id`, `subsubcategory_id`, `brand_id`, `search`.
- Tree: `POST /shop/category/categories_tree` body `{}` — 3 levels, the **third is
  the product type** (and the brand for phones/tablets).
- Detail: `POST /shop/product/get_product?slug=<slug>` body `{"with_variants": true}`.
- Rate limit 120/min; the client self-throttles at 0.55 s/request.

## Price

`price.total` — integer UZS, already discounted. `price.old_price` is the
struck-through figure. One field, through `coerce_price`.

## URL

`https://openshop.uz/shop/product/<slug>?lang=ru`, built from `slug`. **Never the
item's `link`** — it points at `web.openshop.uz`, the API host. Without `?lang=ru`
the page is Uzbek. The page `<title>` states the price (`… | Цена: 4 899 000 сум`);
`page_title(slug)` returns `(title, price)` for verification.

## Traps (each returns HTTP 200)

- `per_page` **capped at 48**, silently.
- **Deep paging caps at ~2,400**: an unfiltered walk said 5,731 and repeated
  products after page 50. Walk per top-level category (`iter_catalogue`).
- `/shop/category/<slug>` in the JS bundle is a front-end route; on the API host it
  returns an empty list.
- `search_product?q=` (header search) is fuzzy over SEO tags and capped at 60 —
  useless for identity.
- Every colour × memory is its own product (`group.id` ties them). iPhones come as
  `(SIM + eSIM)`, `(eSIM + eSIM)`, `(SIM + SIM)` at different prices, plus
  `Certified Refurbished`.
- Only in-stock goods are listed; "not found" can mean sold out.
- Product pages answer the odd 502 — retry before calling a link dead.
- Matching openshop names hit eight matcher gaps (`SIM + eSIM` → phantom `plus`,
  one-digit models dropped off-phone, `/` cuts appliance codes, Uzbek `қ`…): see
  `docs/parsers/openshop.md` §8 before matching outside phones.

Fixture: `tests/fixtures/sources/openshop.json`. Tests: `tests/sources/test_openshop.py`.



================================================
FILE: skills/fetch-radius/SKILL.md
================================================
---
name: fetch-radius
description: Use when fetching prices or listings from radius.uz, querying its Saleor GraphQL, when radius product links 404, or when iPhones and MacBooks are missing from a radius sweep.
---

# radius.uz — Saleor GraphQL, one row per variant

Cleanest source, smallest catalogue (~274 offers) and 4.8% of all
comparisons: judge a source by the second prices it creates. Notes:
`docs/parsers/radius.md`. Client: `narxnoma-crawler/narxnoma/sources/radius.py`.

## Run

```bash
cd narxnoma-crawler   # pip install -e . once
python -m narxnoma.cli fetch --source radius --limit 50
python -m narxnoma.cli fetch --source radius --query "galaxy a26"
```

## Endpoint

```
POST https://new.api.radius.uz/graphql/
Content-Type: application/json
Accept-Language: ru
Origin: https://radius.uz
Referer: https://radius.uz/
```

No auth. `api.radius.uz` does not resolve; `new.api.` is live. Found in the
Next.js chunks, not the network tab (site is server-rendered). GET serves a
Playground; schema is Saleor's. Queries `CATEGORY_QUERY` / `SEARCH_QUERY` in
the client; cursor pagination, `PAGE_SIZE = 100`. `Category.products` already
includes descendants.

## The variant is the offer

`Product.name` = `Samsung Galaxy A26`; `Variant.name` = `Смартфон Samsung
Galaxy A26 5G 8/256GB Black (Черный)`. Identity lives on the variant: emit
one listing per variant, `name_raw` from the variant, price from
`pricing.priceRange.start.amount` (plain UZS), `isAvailable` for stock.

Ids are base64 `Type:number` (`UHJvZHVjdFZhcmlhbnQ6ODA0Ng==` = `ProductVariant:8046`).
A malformed id costs one listing, not the sweep.

## URL — default variant id, not product id

`https://radius.uz/ru/product/<variant-id>-<product-slug>`. Product-id URLs
**404 on every product**; both ids sit beside the slug and both look right.
A non-default variant id also resolves and renders that colour.

## Category slugs — the Apple trap

`noutbuki` holds 9 laptops, none Apple; MacBooks and iPhones live under a
top-level `apple` category, absent from `smartfony`. Slugs are tuples per
category in the client so Apple is swept.

## Traps

- GraphQL failure is **HTTP 200** with `errors` and null `data`; raise on it
  and on a null `category`.
- `thumbnail(size:N).url` is non-null even when the file was never rendered:
  `.avif` sources 404. For avif only, swap `/thumbnails/<N>/` for `/media/`.

Fixtures: `tests/fixtures/sources/radius.json`, `radius_phones.json`. Tests: `tests/sources/test_radius*.py`.



================================================
FILE: skills/fetch-texnomart/SKILL.md
================================================
---
name: fetch-texnomart
description: Use when fetching prices or listings from texnomart.uz, or when a texnomart sweep contains cases, tablets and chargers alongside phones.
---

# texnomart.uz — REST GET, single seller, broad-query sweep

Notes: `docs/parsers/texnomart.md`. Client: `narxnoma-crawler/narxnoma/sources/texnomart.py`.

## Run

```bash
cd narxnoma-crawler   # pip install -e . once
python -m narxnoma.cli fetch --source texnomart --limit 50
python -m narxnoma.cli fetch --source texnomart --category laptops
python -m narxnoma.cli fetch --source texnomart --query "iphone 16 pro"
```

## Endpoint

```
GET https://gw.texnomart.uz/api/common/v1/search/result?q=<text>&page=<1-based>&limit=20
accept-device: web
accept-region: 2
Accept-Language: uz
Origin: https://texnomart.uz
Referer: https://texnomart.uz/
```

No auth. Paginate until `data.pagination.total_page`. No catalogue browse:
`sweep()` sends one broad category word (`CATEGORY_QUERY`: `smartfon`,
`televizor`, `noutbuk`, `konditsioner`, `xolodilnik`, `stiraln`, `duxovka`)
and walks the result.

## Price

`sale_price` only. `old_price` is frequently `0` and must never be a fallback.
Integer UZS.

## URL

`https://texnomart.uz/product/detail/<id>/` from `id`. `image` is the picture.

## Category — a denylist, not an allowlist

Items carry **no category field**. On a 467-offer phone sweep, 30% were not
phones, and 15 of those formed full product keys (a tablet has brand, memory
and colour). Phones use `sources/not_a_phone.py`, a denylist of product-type
nouns: an allowlist would drop any phone whose title lacks the expected noun,
and losing a real phone is worse than admitting an accessory the matcher can
still reject. Non-phone categories keep only titles carrying the category
noun (`CATEGORY_KEEP_WORD`).

Two false positives already handled: `Черное стекло` (protector vs colour —
only the qualified protector sense counts) and `USB-адаптер` in a phone's
marketing title (charger in the box, so `adapter` is not denied).

Fixture: `tests/fixtures/sources/texnomart.json`. Tests: `tests/sources/test_texnomart*.py`, `test_not_a_phone.py`.



================================================
FILE: skills/fetch-uzum/SKILL.md
================================================
---
name: fetch-uzum
description: Use when fetching prices or listings from uzum.uz (Uzum Market), minting its anonymous GraphQL token, or when an uzum request returns 401, HTTP 200 with an errors key, or prices that do not match the product page.
---

# uzum.uz — GraphQL, anonymous token, multi-seller

Largest source (5,000+ offers). Only source with auth, per-SKU pricing, and a
failure mode that looks like success. Full notes: `docs/parsers/uzum.md`.
Client: `narxnoma-crawler/narxnoma/sources/uzum.py`.

## Run

```bash
cd narxnoma-crawler   # pip install -e . once
python -m narxnoma.cli fetch --source uzum --category smartphones --limit 50
python -m narxnoma.cli fetch --source uzum --query "galaxy a56"
```

## Auth — mint, no account

```
POST https://id.uzum.uz/api/auth/token   body {}
Accept-Language: ru-RU        # required (any value)
Referer: https://uzum.uz/     # required (uzum.uz origin)
```

Response **204 No Content**; token is in `Set-Cookie: access_token=<JWT>`, not
the body. `Origin` is NOT required. TTL 6 h (`exp` claim); refresh ~5 min early.
A bare 401 mid-run means server-side revocation: re-mint once, retry once.

## Endpoint

```
POST https://graphql.uzum.uz/
Authorization: Bearer <token>
Content-Type: application/json
apollographql-client-name: web-customers
x-iid: <any UUID>
```

All four required. Drop `apollographql-client-name` or `x-iid` and you get
**HTTP 200, `data: null`, `errors: [...]`**. Always inspect `errors`.
Introspection is on; persisted queries not enforced. Query text: `SEARCH_QUERY`
in the client; pagination `offset`/`limit` (72 per page, offset cap 1000, so the
frontier is per category, not per brand).

## Price — SKU, never card

| field | meaning | use |
|---|---|---|
| `skuList[].sellPrice` | this variant's price, integer UZS | **yes** |
| `minSellPrice` | card minimum across every model and tier | no |
| `fullPrice` | struck-through pre-discount | no |

One listing per SKU. Card titles are marketing blurbs bundling several models;
memory and colour come from SKU `characteristics`, whose keys are typo-ridden
(`Объем встроенной памяти` vs `Объём ...`) — match by token `ram`/`rom`, not by
exact key. Category filter client-side on `catalogCard.category.id`
(15271 Android, 15272 iPhone; other categories in `CATEGORY_IDS`).

## URL

`https://uzum.uz/ru/product/<productId>?skuId=<skuId>` — 302s to the slug URL;
never build a slug. `?skuId=` preselects the exact variant.

## Images

Request `photos` explicitly; it is not in the default selection. Missing it
published 5,461 offers with no picture while every count stayed green.

## Traps

- eSIM-only variants carry a marker; an unmarked phone is dual-SIM, not unknown.
- Colours in characteristics are Russian; in card/model strings often Uzbek
  (`Kumush`, `Tilla`, `Yashil`, `Muz`). Both vocabularies in the matcher.
- Search is strict AND: drop colour words and `5G`/`4G` from the query.
- Multi-seller: many partners per SKU. Price = IQR-trimmed median; uzum also
  reports a min.

Fixture: `tests/fixtures/sources/uzum.json`, `uzum_photos.json`. Tests: `tests/sources/test_uzum*.py`.



================================================
FILE: skills/narxnoma-excel-workbooks/SKILL.md
================================================
---
name: narxnoma-excel-workbooks
description: Use when generating an Excel workbook that must be analysable, not static - PivotTables and slicers from Python, Excel Tables as a database, formulas that follow edits, conditional-formatting heat maps with per-row baselines, Data Model / DAX, or verifying a generated .xlsx in real Excel. Applies to narxnoma research workbooks and any openpyxl-built file.
---

# Analysable Excel workbooks from Python

Full write-up with every trap: `docs/excel-workbooks.md`. Working code:
`narxnoma-crawler/narxnoma/research/` (`workbook.py`, `excel/pivots.ps1`,
`excel/ooxml_pivot.py`, `excel/verify.ps1`). Verified against desktop Excel 16.

## The order (never break it)

`openpyxl builds` → `pivots / slicers / Data Model added` → `nothing re-saves via openpyxl`.
openpyxl cannot make a PivotTable and silently drops slicers and the Data Model on save.

## Checklist

1. **Database = Excel Tables** (one fact per row, flat long format). Table column names must equal
   the header cells — after `table._initialise_columns()` restore them, or Excel refuses the file.
   Calculated columns: `TableFormula(attr_text=…)` without `=`, `Tbl[[#This Row],[Col]]`, so added
   rows fill themselves. Header names without `( ) % # [ ]`.
2. **Knobs = defined names** on a `Settings` sheet (+ data validation). Inputs yellow, rest formulas.
3. **Formulas**: `_xlfn.MINIFS` / `_xlfn.MAXIFS` / `_xlfn.IFS` need the prefix (else `#NAME?`). `""` for
   no data, never 0. openpyxl writes no cached values → `fullCalcOnLoad`; to bake values let Excel save.
4. **Heat map = conditional formatting**, never painted fills. Per-row baseline = formula rule,
   relative row / absolute column, thresholds from names; both `fgColor` and `bgColor` on the fill;
   `stopIfTrue` for graded bands; `ColorScaleRule(start_type="formula", start_value="-cfg_sat" …)`
   for a scale driven by Settings. On a pivot: `AddColorScale(3)` + `ScopeType = 2`.
5. **COM pivots** (`pivots.ps1`): wrap every call in a retry for `0x800AC472`; `CalculateFull`
   first; `PivotCaches().Create(1, "tblX")`; ONE cache, many pivots, slicers via
   `SlicerCaches.Add2` + `.PivotTables.AddPivotTable`; number formats on the data field; set
   `CompactLayoutRowHeader/ColumnHeader` and `GrandTotalName` (they follow the UI language);
   optional steps in `Try-Step`; `Quit()` in `finally`.
6. **Data Model**: probe `$wb.Model`; `Connections.Add2(…, 7, $true, $false)` per table;
   `ModelRelationships.Add`, `ModelMeasures.Add` (DAX), pivot from `ThisWorkbookDataModel`. In a
   `try`; flat pivots stay as the fallback.
7. **No Excel**: `ooxml_pivot.py` injects cache + records + pivot parts with `refreshOnLoad="1"`.
   Declare `xmlns:r` on the `<pivotCache>` element (openpyxl's `workbook.xml` doesn't on the root).
   No slicers / chart / model.
8. **Verify in Excel**: `verify.ps1` (error cells per sheet, table/pivot/slicer/CF counts,
   `-Probe` displayed fill colour of named cells). Then recompute independently in Python and
   compare. Then render a sheet (`ExportAsFixedFormat` → PDF → PyMuPDF) and **look**. Then change an
   input through COM and re-probe.

## Don't

- Don't claim a workbook works because openpyxl wrote it. Say if Excel never opened it.
- Don't bake a colour that a rule can compute.
- Don't put a business's private figures in examples or fixtures in this public repo: synthetic
  rows only (`tests/research/_synth.py`).



================================================
FILE: skills/narxnoma-fetch/SKILL.md
================================================
---
name: narxnoma-fetch
description: Use when asked for electronics prices, listings or product data from Uzbek online retailers (uzum, alifshop, beemarket, idea, mediapark, olcha, texnomart, asaxiy, radius, glotr, openshop), or when running, extending or debugging the narxnoma crawler engine.
---

# narxnoma fetch engine

## Overview

Eleven verified retailer clients behind one `Source` protocol, one `Listing`
contract, and a CLI that needs no database. Every client was rebuilt from the
notes in `docs/parsers/` and asserted live; the notes are canonical. When code
and doc disagree, verify live and fix both in one commit.

## Quick start (no Postgres)

```bash
pip install -e narxnoma-crawler          # Python >= 3.11; requests + psycopg only
cd narxnoma-crawler
python -m narxnoma.cli fetch --source olcha --limit 20              # sweep, JSON lines
python -m narxnoma.cli fetch --source uzum --category laptops        # default: smartphones
python -m narxnoma.cli fetch --source mediapark --query "iphone 16"  # search-only source
python -m narxnoma.cli fetch --source texnomart --raw > texnomart.jsonl
```

One `Listing` per line: `store_slug, source_key, name_raw, price (integer UZS),
url, availability (IN_STOCK|OUT_OF_STOCK|UNKNOWN), image_url, category`.
`--raw` appends the retailer's untouched payload. `fetch` is raw listings: a
search returns cases and other storage tiers too, so pick the variant by
`name_raw`; the matcher runs only in the Postgres pipeline (`cluster`). Exit 1 when a search-only
source is asked to sweep; never an empty stream that exits 0.

Categories: `smartphones televisions laptops acs fridges washers ovens`.

For a **list of products compared across the markets** (and an Excel file to analyse), do not
loop `fetch` yourself: use `python -m narxnoma.research run` (skill `narxnoma-market-research`).

## Pick the source skill

| slug | kind | sweep | search | skill |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| uzum | GraphQL, anonymous self-minted token, multi-seller | yes | yes | fetch-uzum |
| alifshop | REST POST, multi-seller, price in **tiyin** | yes | yes | fetch-alifshop |
| beemarket | REST GET, category endpoint | yes | yes | fetch-beemarket |
| idea | REST GET, brand queries, no pagination | yes | yes | fetch-idea |
| mediapark | REST GET autocomplete, ~10 hits, **no sweep** | no | yes | fetch-mediapark |
| olcha | REST GET, full catalogue pull | yes | client-side | fetch-olcha |
| texnomart | REST GET, broad query `q=smartfon` | yes | yes | fetch-texnomart |
| asaxiy | server-rendered HTML, `/ru/` path | yes | no | fetch-asaxiy |
| radius | Saleor GraphQL, one row per variant | yes | yes | fetch-radius |
| glotr | server-rendered HTML classifieds, **robots.txt disallows** | by grant only | no | fetch-glotr |
| openshop | REST POST, full catalogue ~120 requests, filters in query string | yes | fuzzy | fetch-openshop |

Each source skill states the exact endpoint, headers, price field and unit,
URL rule, and the traps that silently produce wrong numbers.

## Rules every source obeys

1. **Never trust a slug you built yourself.** Open one built URL in a browser
   before trusting the column (alifshop `/moderated-offer/`, radius default-variant id).
2. **Verify the price against the rendered page**, not only the JSON (uzum card vs SKU).
3. **Search relevance is not precision.** Endpoints return cases, chargers and
   other storage tiers; the matcher decides, not the search engine.
4. **Broad query, strict match.** Strict-AND engines return zero on extra
   words; strip colour/`5G`/brand from the query, enforce them in the matcher.
5. **Price fences**: `100_000 <= price <= 100_000_000` UZS, else it is a unit
   bug (`narxnoma/sources/base.py`). Run every raw price through `coerce_price`.
6. **Be polite**: sequential requests, 0.3–0.6 s pause (`NARXNOMA_MIN_DELAY`/`MAX_DELAY`),
   real `User-Agent`, the `Origin`/`Referer` the site's own SPA sends. A site that
   publishes a rate limit gets its own floor in the client (openshop: 120/min → 0.55 s).
7. **Read the matched names, not the counts.** A new store's naming style is a new
   test corpus for the matcher; see `docs/parsers/openshop.md` §8 for eight gaps
   that passed every automated check.

## Engine layout

```
narxnoma-crawler/narxnoma/
  sources/<slug>.py   Source: slug, can_sweep, sweep(category), search(query)
  sources/base.py     Listing, coerce_price, try_build_listing, paginate
  sources/http.py     polite_session, get_json, get_text, post_json
  matching/           name -> (brand, model, memory, colour) identity, scoring
  pipeline/           harvest -> cluster -> aggregate -> report (Postgres)
  cli.py              fetch | harvest | cluster | aggregate | quality | report | cycle
tests/fixtures/sources/<slug>.json|html   recorded live responses, one per source
docs/parsers/<slug>.md                    canonical notes
```

With Postgres: `export NARXNOMA_DSN=postgresql://...` then
`python -m narxnoma.cli cycle` (see `docs/deployment/local-postgres.md`).

## Tests

```bash
cd narxnoma-crawler && python -m pytest -q            # fixture-backed, offline
python -m pytest -q -m live tests/sources/test_contract_live.py   # hits retailers
```

## Common mistakes

| Mistake | Reality |
|---|---|
| Scrape the HTML | Nine of eleven sites are SPAs with an empty shell; call the JSON API the SPA calls. asaxiy and glotr are the exceptions. |
| Check HTTP status only | uzum and radius report GraphQL failure with **HTTP 200** and an `errors` key. |
| Use `minSellPrice` / `old_price` / `price` | Card, struck-through and list prices are not the offer price. Each skill names the one field. |
| Stop paging on an empty page | asaxiy redirects out-of-range pages to page 1; glotr serves unrelated filler forever. Stop on the site's own pager. |
| Treat count as correctness | A present-but-wrong image/URL keeps every count green. Read the id twice from two places and drop the row when they disagree. |

Adding a store: see skill `adding-a-narxnoma-source`.



================================================
FILE: skills/narxnoma-market-research/SKILL.md
================================================
---
name: narxnoma-market-research
description: Use when asked to research a list of products against Uzbek retailers (uzum, alifshop, beemarket, idea, mediapark, olcha, texnomart, asaxiy, radius, openshop), compare our prices with the market, or deliver an Excel file the user can analyse, pivot and heat-map rather than a static table.
---

# narxnoma market research

A product list in; an **analysable** workbook out — Excel Tables as a database, formulas that
follow edits, a conditional-formatting heat map, real PivotTables. Canonical doc:
`docs/market-research.md`; the Excel engineering: `docs/excel-workbooks.md` (and skill
`narxnoma-excel-workbooks`).

## Run it

```bash
pip install -e "narxnoma-crawler[excel]"          # once; openpyxl for the workbook
cd narxnoma-crawler
python -m narxnoma.research run --targets products.xlsx --out research.xlsx
python -m narxnoma.research run --targets list.txt --category smartphones --sources uzum,olcha --out p.xlsx
python -m narxnoma.research workbook --from research.json --out research.xlsx   # rebuild, no fetching
python -m narxnoma.research check research.xlsx                                  # Excel recalculates it
```

The list: `.xlsx` / `.csv` / `.txt`. Only a name is required. Useful columns: `Our price`
(UZS, or `Currency` = USD), `Category` (smartphones, televisions, laptops, acs, fridges,
washers, ovens — or the RU/UZ word), and **any extra column you want to pivot by** (brand,
supplier, segment): it becomes a column on `Products`.

## Decide before running

1. **Category.** A product with none is not matched. Pass `--category`, or put it in the file.
2. **Markets.** Default = all but glotr (robots.txt forbids; only by grant — name it
   deliberately). `mediapark` is search-only: one query per product.
3. **Pivots.** `--pivots auto` = Excel COM when Excel is installed (slicers, chart, Data Model
   if Power Pivot exists) else raw OOXML (pivots build on open; no slicers). `none` = tables +
   formulas only. Say which one you used.
4. **Excel version.** `MINIFS`/`MAXIFS` need Excel 2019 / 365.

## Rules when presenting results

- **Say what the workbook is**: a database + view; edit yellow cells on `Products` (Our price,
  Category) and thresholds on `Settings`; everything follows. Don't hand over a screenshot or
  CSV of the grid when the workbook exists.
- **Colour direction**: red = the market is *cheaper* than the baseline; green = dearer. Baseline
  = our price, else market median (`Settings!cfg_baseline`).
- **An empty cell is "no match", never zero.** Misses are the price of precision (brand, model,
  RAM and storage must match; colour is not identity except iPhone). Known matcher gaps
  (`docs/parsers/openshop.md` §8) show as misses, not wrong prices.
- **Read `Name delta` on `Offers` before quoting an outlier.** A plausible price is not a right
  one; the listing name is checkable.
- **Freshness**: each market's catalogue time is on `Markets` and `Notes`; a failed sweep is
  named there. Re-matching is free (24 h cache); `--force` refetches.
- **Verify, don't assume**: `run` / `workbook` call Excel's recalculation check when Excel is
  present and exit 1 on any formula error. Without Excel, say the file is unverified.
- Never re-save the finished file through openpyxl (drops slicers and the Data Model).

## Extending

New store → `adding-a-narxnoma-source` (it appears in research automatically once registered in
`SOURCES`; add it to `collect.MARKET_ORDER`). A new input alias → `targets.ALIASES` /
`CATEGORY_ALIASES`. Workbook layout → `research/workbook.py`; add or change a test in
`tests/research/test_research_workbook.py` (and run `pytest -m excel` on a machine with Excel).

# tools/

Maintenance scripts for this repo. Read this when you need to regenerate generated
files or run a recurring repo chore. The engine's own commands live in
`narxnoma-crawler/narxnoma/cli.py`, not here.
